### [82] [Hessian-augmented Supervised Learning for Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23827)
*Matías Gómez-Aedo,Behzad Azmi,Yuyang Huang,Dante Kalise,Karl Kunisch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据驱动方法，利用庞特里亚金最大值原理生成包含值函数、梯度、Hessian的训练数据，通过加权最小二乘回归在稀疏多项式基上逼近值函数，从而恢复反馈控制律。高维问题中采用部分Hessian策略控制数据生成成本。实验表明，二阶数据增强可显著减少训练样本需求并提升逼近精度与闭环性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在确定性最优控制问题中，传统方法面临高维状态空间下值函数难以精确逼近的问题，而仅依赖值函数回归需要大量训练样本。本文旨在通过引入梯度与Hessian信息来降低样本复杂度，提高高维问题中值函数近似的效率与精度。

Method: 基于庞特里亚金最大值原理从多个初始条件求解最优性系统，生成包含值函数、梯度、Hessian的训练数据（Hessian通过沿最优轨迹的矩阵Riccati方程获得）。利用稀疏多项式基（双曲交叉指标集）进行加权最小二乘回归，将梯度与Hessian作为额外的线性方程融入回归过程。从学习得到的值函数解析地恢复反馈控制律。高维场景采用部分Hessian策略控制数据生成成本。

Result: 在多个状态维度的递增问题中验证，二阶数据增强相较于仅值函数回归及一阶方法，能以约一个数量级更少的训练样本达到相当的逼近精度和闭环控制性能，显著提升了样本效率。

Conclusion: 梯度与Hessian信息的引入能有效降低值函数逼近所需的样本复杂度，提升高维最优控制问题的求解效率。部分Hessian策略为高维应用提供了可行的计算方案。

Abstract: A data-driven method is developed for approximating value functions in deterministic optimal control problems with nonlinear control-affine dynamics. The Pontryagin Maximum Principle optimality system is solved from multiple initial conditions to generate training data consisting of values, gradients, and Hessians of the value function, where Hessian information is obtained from a matrix Riccati equation along optimal trajectories. These quantities augment a weighted least-squares regression over sparse polynomial bases on hyperbolic cross index sets, with gradients and Hessians contributing additional linear equations per sample and substantially reducing sample complexity compared to value-only regression. Feedback laws are recovered analytically from the learned value function. In high dimensions, a partial Hessian strategy controls the cost of data generation. The approach is validated on problems of increasing state dimension, where second-order data augmentation is shown to improve approximation accuracy and closed-loop performance, with up to an order-of-magnitude reduction in the number of training samples required relative to lower-order methods.

</details>


### [83] [Computational Framework for Bézier Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23841)
*Esteban Leiva,Andrés L. Medaglia,Luis F. Zuluaga*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于一阶优化方法的贝塞尔分布拟合计算框架，利用保序回归实现高效投影算子，开发了开源Python包bezierv，使计算速度比传统方法快3-4个数量级，同时保持同等精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝塞尔分布虽能表示复杂形状的有界连续单变量分布，但因缺乏高效拟合方法和软件实现而未被广泛采用，亟需弥补这一理论与实践的差距。

Method: 采用最小误差和最大似然估计，利用一阶优化方法并利用参数空间几何结构；通过保序回归识别可证明渐近无损的凸约束可行集，设计高效投影算子。

Result: 与传统的无导数方法相比，计算运行时间减少了3-4个数量级；与非线性求解器IPOPT相比，平均快三个数量级且更鲁棒，精度相当；基于真实数据实现一致的拟合效果。

Conclusion: 开发的计算框架和bezierv开源包有效解决了贝塞尔分布的实际应用瓶颈，为随机模拟和决策分析中的有界分布拟合提供了高效、鲁棒的实用工具。

Abstract: Flexible continuous univariate distributions with bounded support are essential for accurate input modeling in stochastic simulation and decision analysis. Although Bézier distributions provide a powerful family capable of representing complex shapes, their adoption has been hindered by the lack of efficient fitting procedures and modern software implementations. This paper develops a computational framework for fitting Bézier distributions to empirical data via both minimum error and maximum likelihood estimation, leveraging first-order optimization methods and exploiting the geometry of the parameter space. We identify provably (asymptotically) lossless convex restrictions of the feasible set that enable efficient projection operators based on isotonic regression and develop first-order algorithms that reduce computational runtime by three to four orders of magnitude compared to traditional derivative-free methods, while delivering consistent fits across real-world data. When benchmarked against the nonlinear solver IPOPT, our methods prove three orders of magnitude faster on average and more robust, while achieving comparable accuracy. To bridge the gap between theory and practice, we introduce bezierv, an open-source Python package providing a unified interface for fitting, analyzing, and convolving Bézier distributions.

</details>


### [84] [Constrained Variable Projection for Structured Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23939)
*Emanuele Zangrando,Sara Venturini,Francesco Rinaldi,Francesco Tudisco*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种约束变量投影框架，将变量投影技术推广到带凸约束的数据科学模型，推导了与自动微分兼容的精确缩减梯度公式，并提出了条件梯度算法，在稀疏自编码、字典学习、盲反卷积和少样本学习等任务上相比联合优化基线提升了计算效率和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变量投影方法仅适用于无约束的可分非线性最小二乘问题，而数据科学模型中的变量往往受凸约束且来源于下层最小二乘问题，需要将变量投影框架扩展至更一般的双层优化场景。

Method: 将变量投影解释为折叠的双层优化问题，从中推导出与自动微分兼容的精确缩减梯度公式，并提出适用于约束缩减问题的条件梯度算法；在标准光滑性和紧性假设下建立收敛性保证，并讨论结构化下层变量的扩展。

Result: 在稀疏自编码、字典学习、盲反卷积和少样本学习等数值实验中，该方法相比自然联合优化基线提升了壁钟效率和数据效率。

Conclusion: 约束变量投影框架有效地将经典变量投影技术推广至带约束的数据科学模型，自动微分兼容的梯度公式和条件梯度算法使其在实际应用中兼具理论保证和计算优势。

Abstract: Variable projection is a classical technique for separable nonlinear least-squares problems, in which variables that enter linearly are eliminated exactly, yielding a reduced nonlinear problem. By expressing this framework as a particular instance of a broader class of bilevel optimization problems, we develop a constrained variable-projection framework for data-science models, where the remaining variables are subject to convex constraints and the eliminated variables arise from a lower-level least-squares problem. In particular, by interpreting variable projection as a collapsed bilevel optimization problem, we derive exact reduced-gradient formulas compatible with automatic differentiation and propose a conditional-gradient algorithm for the resulting constrained reduced problem. We establish convergence guarantees under standard smoothness and compactness assumptions, and discuss extensions to structured lower-level variables. Numerical experiments on sparse autoencoding, dictionary learning, blind deconvolution, and few-shot learning suggest that the method can improve wall-clock efficiency and data efficiency relative to natural joint-optimization baselines.

</details>


### [85] [A Lorentzian Lasry-Lions regularization theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23976)
*Alec Metsch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了全局双曲时空中Lorentzian Lasry-Lions正则化定理，证明在特定条件下Lax-Oleinik演化的复合函数具有C^{1,1}_{loc}正则性，并将其应用于最优传输理论中位移插值的对偶表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全局双曲时空背景下，建立Lorentzian版本的Lasry-Lions正则化定理，以处理函数在因果结构下的正则性提升问题。

Method: 利用前向Lax-Oleinik演化Tu的局部半凹性及未来指向类时超微分性质，通过复合算子\hat T_s\circ T_tu构造C^{1,1}_{loc}正则函数。

Result: 对于接近t_0的时间t和足够小的s>0，函数\hat T_s\circ T_tu在y_0邻域内属于C^{1,1}_{loc}类；在最优传输中，位移插值的中间测度间存在C^{1,1}_{loc}正则的最大化对。

Conclusion: 给出了保证定理成立的充分条件，并展示了该结果在最优传输对偶理论中的具体应用。

Abstract: The main goal of this paper is to establish a general Lorentzian Lasry-Lions regularization theorem: let $u$ be a function defined on a globally hyperbolic spacetime. Assume that its forward Lax--Oleinik evolution $Tu$ is locally semiconcave in a neighbourhood of $(t_0,y_0)$ and has future-directed timelike superdifferentials there. Then, for $t$ close to $t_0$ and sufficiently small $s>0$, the function $\hat T_s\circ T_tu$ is of class $C_{\mathrm{loc}}^{1,1}$ in a neighbourhood of $y_0$. We give sufficient conditions ensuring the assumptions of the theorem and present an application to optimal transport: under quite general assumptions, for any two intermediate measures along a displacement interpolation, there exists a $C^{1,1}_{loc}$-regular maximizing pair in the dual formulation.

</details>


### [86] [Traveling profiles and control cost for a PDE describing the evolution of invasive species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23979)
*Stefano Bianchini,Chiara Trifone*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对入侵物种控制模型中的最优行波问题，证明了在相平面中存在唯一的最优轮廓，给出了显式的一阶最优性条件，分析了控制的可积性和光滑性，并刻画了最小控制努力函数关于速度β和反应项f的凸性、正则性和渐近性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为入侵物种控制模型（Bressan, Chiri, Salehi, 2022）中的最优行波问题提供严格的理论分析，特别是最小化控制努力（L1范数）所需的最优控制策略的数学结构。

Method: 采用相平面分析方法，在(U, P=U_x)相空间中研究最优控制轮廓P_β(U)，通过变分法推导一阶最优性条件，并利用反应扩散方程的结构分析控制函数的支撑集和正则性。

Result: 1) 相平面中存在唯一的最优轮廓；2) 显式的一阶最优性条件既是必要的也是充分的；3) 最优控制作用在相空间的一个开子集上，均匀可积，且在稠密开集上光滑依赖于β和f；4) 努力函数E(β,f)关于β仅为C1，关于f在C2拓扑下Lipschitz连续，且当β→∞时渐近线性；5) E(β,f)关于β一般不凸也不次可加。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了入侵物种控制模型中最小化控制努力的行波解结构，为大规模动力学分析奠定了理论基础，揭示了努力函数的非凸性和有限光滑性等关键数学性质。

Abstract: We develop a detailed analysis of optimal traveling waves $U(t,x) = U(x - βt)$ for a model of invasive-species control proposed in [Bressan, Chiri, and Salehi, Math. Models Methods Appl. Sci., 2022] : the relative density $U \in [0,1]$ of the invasive species satisfies the following reaction-diffusion equation with a positive control \begin{equation} \label{Equa:PDE_abstract} U_t = U_{xx} + f(U) - \tilde α(t,x) U, \quad U \in [0,1], \ \tilde α\geq 0. \end{equation} The control $\tilde α(t,x)$ represents the fraction of the population removed at $(t,x)$: the minimal control effort $E(β,f)$ required to sustain a traveling invasion front with prescribed speed $β$ is defined as the minimal $L^1$-norm of $\tilde α$ for a traveling wave solution $U(x-βt)$ to the PDE. In order to study large scale dynamics $(t,x) \mapsto (εt,εx)$, a fundamental role is played by the structure of traveling waves and the convexity and regularity properties of $E$. The main results of this paper are the following: 1)In the phase plane $(U,P=U_x)$, there exists a unique optimal profile $P_β(U)$ minimizing the effort; 2) It satisfies explicit first-order conditions, which are both necessary and sufficient; 3) The associated control is acting on an open subset of the set $\{U : P_β(U) = \sqrt{U f(U)}\}$, in particular it is uniformly integrable, and it depends smoothly on $(β,f)$ on a dense open set; 4)The effort function $E(β,f)$ is only $C^1$ w.r.t. $β$ and Lipschitz w.r.t. $f$ in the $C^2$-topology, and is asymptocally linear for $β\to \infty$; 5)$β\mapsto E(β,f)$ is in general neither convex nor subadditive.

</details>


### [87] [Distributionally Robust Joint Information and Mechanism Design for Multi-Area Power System Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24015)
*Furkan Sezer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间随机Stackelberg控制问题，领导者通过公共信号渠道和Groves转移机制（而非直接动力学）引导策略性追随者。在跳扩散潜在环境下，利用有限维投影滤波器跟踪信念，证明激励对齐使双层问题简化为单层鲁棒控制问题，得到精确一阶条件，并刻画值函数为唯一粘性解。基于2021年冬季风暴Uri校准的ERCOT多区域电力系统实验表明，公共信息披露可降低37-48%的福利成本，信息设计与市场耦合在系统性风险下具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Stackelberg博弈中领导者通过直接动力学控制系统，但许多实际问题中（如电力系统协调、市场机制设计）领导者只能通过信息结构和转移支付间接影响追随者行为。现有研究缺乏处理连续时间、跳扩散环境、非标准控制渠道以及分布鲁棒性问题的统一理论框架。

Method: 构建连续时间随机Stackelberg博弈模型，领导者设计高斯公共信号（其信念更新由有限维投影滤波器跟踪）和Groves转移机制。在诚实披露下证明有效行为是占优策略。将领导者的分布鲁棒问题（基于相对熵模糊性邻域）转化为双控制器的Isaacs方程，证明激励对齐将双层问题坍缩为单层鲁棒控制问题，得到精确一阶条件。通过粘性解理论和验证定理处理非光滑bang-bang反馈，并证明半凹性使切换集为零测集。

Result: 激励对齐后双层Stackelberg问题等价于单层鲁棒控制问题。ERCOT系统实验显示：互相援助可降低约8%的社会成本，在FERC/DOE推荐的跨区域传输能力下升至约30%；公共信息披露在自给自足和市场耦合下分别降低37%和48%的福利成本；信息设计与市场耦合在共同（系统性）风险下呈互补关系。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论框架有效处理了领导者通过信息结构和转移机制间接控制的连续时间随机Stackelberg问题，数值实验验证了公共信息披露和跨区域电力交易在提升系统韧性方面的显著互补性，为极端天气下电力系统协调提供了理论支持和量化依据。

Abstract: We study a continuous-time stochastic Stackelberg control problem in which a leader steers a system of strategic followers through two non-standard channels - the information structure and a transfer mechanism - rather than through the dynamics directly. The latent environment is a jump-diffusion; the leader commits to a Gaussian public-signaling channel whose belief consequences are tracked by a finite-dimensional projection filter (the exact filter being infinite-dimensional), together with a Groves transfer that aligns the followers' incentives. Under truthful disclosure, efficient behavior is a dominant-strategy best response, and the induced differential game admits saturated and bang-bang Nash feedback. We cast the leader's distributionally robust problem, over a relative-entropy ambiguity neighborhood, as a two-controller Isaacs equation; prove that incentive alignment collapses the bilevel Stackelberg problem to a single robust control problem with an exact first-order condition; and characterize the value function as the unique viscosity solution, with a verification theorem valid for the non-smooth bang-bang feedback and a semiconcavity result that renders the switching set Lebesgue-null. We instantiate the framework on resilient multi-area power-system coordination under extreme weather. Calibrated to the 2021 Winter Storm Uri, an Isaacs solve over ERCOT's near-islanded interconnection (a 0.82 GW tie, under 2% of peak) shows mutual aid removes about 8% of social cost, rising to roughly 30% under the FERC/DOE-recommended interregional transfer capability; a reserve-scheduling experiment shows that public disclosure lowers welfare cost by 37% under autarky and 48% under market coupling, and that information design and market coupling are complements under common (systemic) risk.

</details>


### [88] [Extreme-Case Distorted Utility under Moment Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24294)
*Zehao Li,Yijie Peng,Hui Shao,Chung-Piaw Teo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究矩约束下分布鲁棒决策中的极端扭曲效用问题，提出基于分位数域的非光滑变分分析方法，推导出闭合形式最优解与极值分布，并通过三个经典例子和AI容量规划问题验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布模糊性下的运营决策（如定价、库存、容量、合同）常通过尾部敏感的扭曲效用评估动作，并针对已知矩约束下的最不利分布进行对冲，但现有方法对非光滑、非凸非凹的效用函数和一般扭曲（可能原子化）缺乏统一处理框架。

Method: 将问题重新表述到分位数域，利用非光滑变分分析推导一阶最优性条件和闭合形式的极值/最劣值及分布；通过等距投影处理单调性约束到单调锥，将抽象无限维约束转化为线性可扩展的内部求解。

Result: 恢复了并扩展了三个经典矩界：Scarf界的范围VaR扩展、GlueVaR扭曲与奖惩效用、条件风险价值下的有上限激励合同；在生成式AI推理容量规划中，考虑矩模糊性可降低所需容量同时保持服务合规。

Conclusion: 所提方法为矩约束分布鲁棒决策提供了统一的理论框架，其闭合形式解可直接嵌入外部鲁棒优化，在实际问题中验证了降低保守性的价值。

Abstract: Many operations decisions under distributional ambiguity, from pricing and inventory to capacity and contracting, evaluate an action through a tail-sensitive distorted utility of an uncertain payoff and hedge against the least favorable distribution consistent with a few known moments; the resulting worst-case evaluation is the inner problem of a moment-based distributionally robust decision. We study this inner problem, the extreme-case distorted utility under moment constraints, for a locally Lipschitz utility that may be nonsmooth and neither convex nor concave together with a general, possibly atomic, distortion. Recasting the problem in the quantile domain, we develop a unified method that yields exact first-order optimality conditions and closed-form extremal values and distributions for both the worst and best cases, drawing on nonsmooth variational analysis. A central step treats the monotonicity constraint by isotonic projection onto the monotone cone, turning an abstract infinite-dimensional restriction into an inexpensive inner solve that scales linearly in the discretization. The method recovers and extends classical moment bounds through three examples: a range value-at-risk extension of the Scarf bound, GlueVaR distortions with a reward--penalty utility, and a capped incentive contract under conditional value-at-risk. As the inner oracle of a robust min-max decision, the characterization embeds directly in outer robust optimization, illustrated on a real capacity-provisioning problem for generative artificial intelligence inference where accounting for moment ambiguity lowers required capacity while preserving service compliance.

</details>


### [89] [A multi-objective perspective on block-structured integer programs with one soft coupling constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24318)
*Mark Lyngesen,Kathrin Klamroth,Britta Efkes,Sune Lauth Gadegaard*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多视角方法处理带单个软耦合约束的块结构整数规划，通过将耦合约束转化为第二目标，引入二分搜索定位感兴趣区域，并利用λ查找机制加速计算，显著减少整数规划调用次数，优于传统二分搜索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的块结构整数规划中软耦合约束的处理通常计算成本高昂，完整的帕累托前沿计算过于昂贵，且现有方法无法高效提供最优解的可证明边界。

Method: 1. 将软耦合约束转化为第二目标，构建可加性可分离的双目标优化问题；2. 引入基于二分搜索的算法定位感兴趣区域，提供单目标最优解的可证明边界；3. 利用块结构提出新颖的λ查找机制，避免重复子问题计算；4. 针对需要所有非支配解的场景，提出从感兴趣区域中间向外扩展的新算法。

Result: 计算研究表明该方法大幅减少整数规划调用次数，优于传统二分搜索；在大规模实例中作为强启发式方法，能提供最优解间隙的边界以及额外的权衡信息。

Conclusion: 本文提出的多目标视角方法在保持可证明边界的前提下，显著提升了带软耦合约束的块结构整数规划的计算效率，并提供了传统方法无法获得的权衡信息。

Abstract: This paper presents a multi-objective perspective on block-structured integer programs featuring a single soft coupling constraint. By interpreting the coupling constraint as a second objective, we transform the coupled single-objective problem into an additively-separable bi-objective optimization problem. To avoid the expensive computation of the full Pareto front, we introduce an algorithm, which uses a binary search to isolate a region of interest around the soft constraint limit. This algorithm provides provable bounds on the single-objective optimum. We further enhance this algorithm, by exploiting the block-structure, using a novel $λ$-lookup mechanism to skip repeated sub-problem calculations. Finally, for scenarios requiring all non-dominated solutions within the region of interest, we propose a new approach, that works its way from the middle of the region of interest outwards. This algorithm shows quick convergence in terms of representation. Computational studies demonstrate that our methods dramatically reduce integer programming calls, thereby outperforming traditional dichotomic search. For large instances the method works as a strong heuristic providing bounds on the gap to an optimal solution, providing trade-off information in addition to the solution.

</details>


### [90] [Line Planning at Scale: Models, Methods and Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24386)
*Rolf van Lieshout,Bart van Rossum*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对比了传统换乘网络（CGN）与三种紧凑模型在轨道交通线路规划问题上的表现，发现CGN仅在小规模场景有竞争力，而直接连接模型在83%以上的实例中表现最佳，表明精确换乘建模并非大规模线路规划的最优选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线路规划依赖换乘网络（CGN）精确建模乘客换乘行为，但其规模随线路增长急剧膨胀，难以求解大规模问题。为此，研究者希望探索更紧凑的近似模型，在求解效率与建模精度之间取得平衡。

Method: 在荷兰和瑞士铁路网络的972个实例上，对比CGN与三种紧凑模型（在换乘近似方式上不同）；为每种模型开发了最先进的求解方法，并进行系统性评估。

Result: CGN仅在小规模或简单实例上有竞争力，在大规模网络上常无法找到可行解；直接连接紧凑模型在超过83%的实例上找到了最优解，整体表现最佳。

Conclusion: 面向大规模线路规划，精心设计的近似模型比精确的换乘建模更有前景，直接连接模型可作为替代CGN的有效方案。

Abstract: Line planning, the problem of deciding which lines to operate and at what frequency, is a fundamental step in public transport planning. To accurately model passenger routing, the problem is traditionally defined on a change-and-go network (CGN), which captures transfers between lines exactly. However, this network grows large quickly and is hard to solve at scale. We compare the CGN against three more compact models, differing with respect to how transfers are approximated, and characterize how they relate in terms of solution quality and modeling accuracy. We develop state-of-the-art solution methods tailored to each model, and evaluate all four across 972 instances based on the Dutch and Swiss railway networks. Contrary to the CGN's canonical status, we find that it is competitive only on small or easy instances and often fails to find any feasible solution on large networks. Instead, a compact direct connection model performs best overall, finding the best solution on over 83\% of instances. Our results indicate that carefully designed approximations, rather than exact transfer modeling, are the more promising foundation for large-scale line planning.

</details>


### [91] [Trade-off invariance for weighted scalarizations in multi-objective optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24493)
*Jona Klemenc,Alessandro Scagliotti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究抽象多目标最小化问题的加权和标量化，在无拓扑、凸性、紧致性或下半连续假设下，证明权重的"权衡不变原理"导致目标空间中的通有唯一性：对几乎所有正权重向量，标量化的所有极小点共享同一目标向量，且所有极小化序列收敛到同一极限目标向量。几何上，这表示几乎所有正权重向量最多暴露一个非支配点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏拓扑、凸性、紧致性等常规假设的抽象多目标优化中，加权和标量化的唯一性性质尚未被充分揭示。本文旨在不依赖这些强假设的情况下，建立权重空间中的通有唯一性结果。

Method: 以开单纯形作为正权重的参数空间，利用标量化的"权衡不变原理"（Trade-off Invariance Principle），通过测度论和通有性分析方法，证明几乎所有权重向量下的唯一性性质。

Result: (1) 对几乎所有权重向量，加权和标量化的所有极小点对应同一目标向量；(2) 排除零测集后，所有极小化序列确定相同的极限目标向量；(3) 在可达目标集中，几乎所有正权重向量最多暴露一个非支配点，且极小化序列最多确定一个渐近暴露目标向量。

Conclusion: 在极其一般的抽象多目标优化框架下（无任何拓扑或凸性假设），加权和标量化具有通有唯一性，这为多目标优化的理论分析提供了基础性保证。

Abstract: We consider weighted-sum scalarizations for an abstract multi-objective minimization problem defined by the vector-valued map $U\ni u\mapsto ( f_1(u),\ldots, f_N(u))$, where $U$ is an arbitrary nonempty set and no topology, convexity, compactness, or lower semicontinuity assumption is imposed. Using the open simplex as parameter space for positive weights, we show that the Trade-off Invariance Principle for scalarizations yields a generic uniqueness property in the objective space. Namely, for almost every weight vector, all minimizers of the corresponding weighted-sum scalarization have the same objective vector. Moreover, excluding again a null-measure subset, all minimizing sequences determine the same limiting objective vector, independently of the chosen sequence. We also give a geometric interpretation of these results in the attainable objective set: for almost every positive weight vector, the scalarization exposes at most one nondominated point. Moreover, minimizing sequences determine at most one asymptotically exposed objective vector in the closure of the attainable set.

</details>


### [92] [Variational convexity: new characterizations, calculus rules, and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24545)
*Radu Ioan Bot,Ziyuan Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了变分凸函数的刻画（通过近端包络和上图）、保持变分凸性的运算（非线性/线性复合、求和、近端平均），并将其应用于非线性规划问题，在目标函数和约束条件更弱的假设下建立了局部极小点的保证条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分凸性是Rockafellar于2019年提出的广义凸性概念，能够保证非凸优化问题的稳定点具有局部最优性。本文旨在系统刻画变分凸函数、研究其保持运算，并将其应用于更一般的非线性规划问题，以扩大局部极小点的可保证范围。

Method: 通过近端包络和上图给出变分凸函数的刻画；分析非线性复合、线性复合、求和、近端平均等运算对变分凸性的保持性；将结果应用于含非光滑目标、连续可微不等式约束和仿射等式约束的非线性规划问题。

Result: 建立了变分凸函数的新刻画，证明了多种运算保持变分凸性，并在比现有结果（通常要求目标与约束二次连续可微）更弱的条件下给出了非线性规划问题中局部极小点的判定条件。

Conclusion: 本文推广了变分凸性的理论与应用，能够在不要求二次连续可微性的情形下确保非凸优化问题的局部极小点，显著扩展了现有方法的适用范围。

Abstract: Introduced by R.T. Rockafellar in 2019, variational convexity is a generalized notion of convexity under which stationary points of nonconvex optimization problems can still be guaranteed to exhibit local optimality. In this paper, we provide characterizations of variationally convex functions through their proximal hulls and epigraphs, and investigate operations that preserve variational convexity, including nonlinear and linear composition, summation, and proximal averaging. We further apply these results to identify variational convexity in nonlinear programming problems with possibly nonsmooth objectives, continuously differentiable inequality constraints, and affine equality constraints. This leads to new conditions ensuring local minimizers, rather than merely stationary points, for such problems, extending beyond current state-of-the-art results that typically require twice continuously differentiable objectives and constraints.

</details>


### [93] [Decomposition-Based Constructive Heuristics for the Linear Ordering Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24558)
*Kazutoshi Ando,Tatsuya Sugimoto,Noriyoshi Sukegawa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对线性排序问题（LOP）提出了一种统一的递归构造式启发式框架，研究了三种递归划分原则（基于强连通分量的层次图方法、基于割的最小割方法、基于分数的递归Borda方法），证明了递归Borda方法满足Condorcet准则而Borda规则不满足，实验表明递归Borda方法在构造式启发式中平均解质量最优，但经插入局部搜索后各方法差异显著缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性排序问题（LOP）是经典的NP-hard组合优化问题，现有的构造式启发式方法各有优劣，缺乏统一的框架进行比较和分析，且其理论性质（如Condorcet准则）尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出一个统一的递归框架，将指标集启发式地划分为更小的子集，精确求解足够小的子问题，然后将所得排列拼接。在该框架下研究了三种递归划分原则：基于强连通分量的层次图方法、基于割的最小割方法、基于分数的递归Borda方法。

Result: 递归Borda方法满足Condorcet准则而Borda规则不满足；层次图方法和最小割方法满足更强的Condorcet型性质；在xLOLIB实例上，递归Borda方法在构造式启发式中平均解质量最优；经插入局部搜索后各方法最终解质量差异大幅缩小。

Conclusion: 所提出的递归框架为LOP的构造式启发式提供了统一视角，递归Borda方法在构造阶段表现优异，但局部搜索可以弥补不同构造方法的初始质量差距，使得最终解质量趋同。

Abstract: The linear ordering problem (LOP) is a classical NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem. In this paper, we study constructive heuristics for the LOP. We propose a unified recursive framework in which the index set is heuristically partitioned into smaller subsets, sufficiently small subproblems are solved exactly, and the resulting permutations are concatenated. Within this framework, we investigate three different recursive partitioning principles: a rule based on strongly connected components (the level graph method), a cut-based rule (the minimum cut method), and a score-based rule (the recursive Borda method). We first show that the recursive Borda method satisfies the Condorcet criterion, whereas the Borda rule does not. The level graph method and the minimum cut method satisfy a stronger Condorcet-type property, which the recursive Borda method does not satisfy. Computational experiments on xLOLIB instances show that the recursive Borda method provides the best average solution quality among the tested constructive heuristics. They also show that, after applying insertion-based local search, the differences in final solution quality among these heuristics become much smaller.

</details>


### [94] [Bridging Identification and Second-Order Acceleration: A Fast Alternating Minimization Framework for Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24600)
*Zihao Xia,Min Tao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合近端梯度和立方正则牛顿更新的交替最小化框架，该方法在低维子空间上操作，在KL性质下具有全局收敛性、有限识别性和O(ε^{-3/2})的迭代复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一类包含光滑函数和可能非凸非光滑正则子的复合优化问题，现有方法在处理非凸非光滑目标时收敛性保证不足或计算效率低下。

Method: 提出交替最小化框架，融合近端梯度步和限制在动态识别低维子空间上的立方正则牛顿更新，结合基于KL指数的自适应阈值策略。

Result: 在KL性质下证明全局收敛到稳定点；无需非退化假设即证明有限识别性质；达到O(ε^{-3/2})的最坏情况迭代复杂度以实现近似二阶稳定点；数值实验验证有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效处理非凸非光滑复合优化问题，兼具理论收敛保证和实际计算效率。

Abstract: We consider a class of composite optimization problems involving a smooth function and a proper, lower semicontinuous regularizer, which may be nonconvex and nonsmooth.
  We propose a novel alternating minimization framework that integrates proximal-gradient steps with cubic-regularized Newton updates restricted to a dynamically identified low-dimensional subspace.
  Under the Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz (KL) property, we establish global convergence of the proposed method to a stationary point.
  Moreover, by incorporating an adaptive thresholding strategy guided by the KL exponent, we prove a finite identification property without imposing any nondegeneracy assumptions.
  We further develop a local convergence analysis and show that the proposed method attains a worst-case iteration complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-3/2})$ for achieving approximate second-order stationarity.
  Numerical experiments on both synthetic and real datasets demonstrate the efficiency and effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [95] [Parallel Dynamic Programming for Conic Linear Quadratic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24632)
*Luyao Zhang,Gabriel Bravo-Palacios,Brian Plancher,Sergio Grammatico*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于ADMM的时域并行方法，通过将LQ问题沿时间轴分割并利用动态规划推导Riccati递归的变体来并行求解每个子问题，在真实世界应用中实现了相比现有方法高达5倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LQ控制问题的求解方法本质上是串行的，尽管现代多核CPU具备并行计算能力，但实时可扩展性仍然受限。为解决这一挑战，受"分而治之"策略启发，作者希望提出一种时域并行方法来加速求解计算密集的锥最优控制问题。

Method: 将ADMM的内部原始更新 formulated 为LQ问题，沿时间轴分割该问题，并利用动态规划推导Riccati递归的变体，使得每个子问题可以并行求解。

Result: 在两个真实世界应用的数值基准测试中，在多核CPU硬件上实现了相比现有相关方法高达5倍的加速比。

Conclusion: 所提出的并行-in-time方法能有效利用现代多核CPU的并行计算能力，显著加速求解锥最优控制问题，为解决LQ及MPC中的实时可扩展性难题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Linear Quadratic (LQ) control problems are at the heart of linear control theory and Model Predictive Control (MPC). While performant, standard approaches to solving such problems are inherently serial, limiting real-time scalability despite the parallel computing power available on modern multi-core CPUs. Contributing to addressing this challenge and motivated by ``divide and conquer'' strategies, we present a parallel-in-time approach that solves computationally demanding conic optimal control problems through the use of the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM). In particular, we formulate the inner primal update of ADMM as an LQ problem and split the reformulated problem along the time horizon. This enables us to derive a variant of the Riccati recursion using dynamic programming to solve each subproblem in parallel. Numerical benchmarks on two real-world applications demonstrate as much as a 5x speedup compared to existing related approaches on multi-core CPU hardware.

</details>


### [96] [Suboptimal and Reduced-Order MPC via Timescale Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24641)
*Stefano Di Gregorio,Guido Carnevale,Giuseppe Notarstefano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用框架，用于次优和降阶非线性模型预测控制（MPC）架构的设计与分析。该框架通过有限次迭代计算次优控制动作并使用忽略部分动态的降阶模型来管理MPC的实时运行，将采样时间作为可调设计参数，利用时间尺度分离工具分析闭环系统，证明了足够快的采样率可确保递归可行性和指数稳定平衡点，并在MuJoCo物理引擎中的欠驱动两连杆机械臂上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性MPC在实际应用中面临在线计算负担重和模型复杂度高的挑战，次优求解与模型简化之间的交互缺乏严格的理论分析，需要一种统一框架来兼顾计算效率与闭环稳定性保证。

Method: 将采样时间作为可调设计参数；将迭代优化算法视为动态系统，与全阶物理对象互联；利用时间尺度分离工具分析闭环系统。

Result: 证明了在足够快采样率下，闭环系统保持递归可行性并达到指数稳定平衡点；在MuJoCo仿真中的欠驱动两连杆机械臂上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 所提通用框架能够严格处理次优求解与模型失配之间的相互影响，为非线性MPC的实时部署提供理论保障。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a generalized framework for the design and analysis of suboptimal and reduced-order nonlinear Model Predictive Control (MPC) architectures. The proposed framework manages real-time operation of MPC schemes by (i) computing the control action suboptimally, i.e., by running a generic optimal control algorithm for a finite number of iterations, and (ii) relying on a reduced-order model that neglects part of the plant dynamics (accounting for, e.g., unmodeled dynamics or a low-level compensator). To rigorously handle the interplay between optimization error and model mismatch, we treat the sampling time as a tunable design parameter. We analyze the resulting closed-loop system, comprising the full-order physical plant interconnected with the iterative optimization algorithm (treated as a dynamical system), by leveraging tools from timescale separation. We prove that operating at a sufficiently fast sampling rate ensures that the closed-loop system maintains recursive feasibility and achieves an exponentially stable equilibrium point. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is validated on an underactuated two-link robotic arm through virtual experiments in the high-fidelity MuJoCo physics engine.

</details>


### [97] [Observer design for Lur'e systems via injection of a reconstructed nonlinear output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24656)
*Adel Malik Annabi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对Lur'e系统观测器设计中增益随耦合强度无限增长的问题，提出通过一组二阶滑模观测器重构关键非线性项并将其注入Luenberger观测器作为额外测量，从而在误差动力学中衰减非线性耦合。新LMI包含经典设计作为特例，在经典设计需要过大增益的参数区间内保持适度增益，并分析了设计与噪声之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Lur'e系统观测器设计依赖LMI求解，在强非线性耦合情况下观测器增益可能趋于无穷大，导致实际应用中不可实现。需要一种能在保持适度增益的同时有效处理强非线性耦合的观测器设计方案。

Method: 采用一组二阶滑模观测器重构系统中的关键非线性项，然后将这些重构信号作为额外测量注入到Luenberger观测器中，从而在误差动力学中引入第二条校正通道来衰减非线性耦合的影响。稳定性分析基于一个新的LMI条件，该条件将经典LMI作为特例包含在内。

Result: 证明了在经典设计需要过大增益的参数区间内，所提设计能保持适度增益；建立了两种设计之间的权衡关系；识别了所提设计更优的噪声区间；通过Wilson-Cowan网络实例展示了非线性耦合强度、观测器增益与噪声之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 所提出的组合观测器设计有效解决了经典Lur'e系统观测器在高耦合强度下增益过大的问题，通过引入非线性项重构与额外校正通道，在保持稳定性的同时实现了增益的适度化，并提供了完整的设计权衡分析。

Abstract: Observer design for Lur'e systems typically reduces to solving a linear matrix inequality (LMI). In certain cases, the observer gain may grow unbounded with the coupling strength. We propose reconstructing key nonlinear terms via a bank of second-order sliding-mode observers and injecting them into a Luenberger observer as additional measurements. This feeds a second correction channel that attenuates the nonlinear coupling in the error dynamics. The convergence of the combined observer is guaranteed under a new LMI which contains the classical one as a special case. We exhibit parameter regimes where classical designs require impractically large gains, while the proposed design maintains moderate gains. A stability analysis of the proposed observer characterizes the trade-off between the two designs and identifies the noise regimes in which the proposed design is preferable. We illustrate the approach on a Wilson-Cowan network showing the trade-off between the nonlinear coupling strength, observer gain, and noise.

</details>


### [98] [New Bounds for the Last Iterate of the Stochastic subGradient Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24879)
*Guglielmo Beretta,Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni,Andrea Paudice*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维凸Lipschitz目标函数下随机次梯度方法（SsGM）的最后迭代的优化误差。对于固定步长η=Θ(1/√n)，在加性i.i.d.次梯度噪声且方差一致有界的条件下，最后迭代的优化误差为O(1/√n)，去掉了现有通用界中的(log n)因子；但若不满足i.i.d.假设，误差可达O((log n)/√n)。这表明在仅有一致有界方差假设下，即使在一维情形下，最后迭代也是次优的，否定了Koren和Segal (COLT 2020)提出的一个开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清随机次梯度方法最后迭代收敛性的精确上界，特别是验证是否存在额外的对数因子，并解决Koren和Segal (COLT 2020)提出的开放问题。

Method: 通过理论分析，分别研究在i.i.d.次梯度噪声和非i.i.d.噪声两种设定下，采用固定步长η=Θ(1/√n)时一维凸Lipschitz目标函数下SsGM最后迭代的优化误差界。

Result: 在i.i.d.噪声下，最后迭代优化误差为O(1/√n)，优于现有通用界；在非i.i.d.但一致有界方差假设下，误差为O((log n)/√n)，表明该设定下最后迭代是次优的。

Conclusion: 一维情形下，仅靠一致有界方差假设不足以使SsGM最后迭代达到最优收敛率，否定了之前文献中提出的开放问题。

Abstract: We study the last iterate of the stochastic subgradient method for one-dimensional convex Lipschitz objectives. For a fixed horizon $n$, we consider the standard fixed stepsizes $η=Θ(1/\sqrt n)$. We prove that, for such stepsize policies, under additive i.i.d. subgradient noise with uniformly bounded variance, the last iterate features an optimization error of order $1/\sqrt n$, thereby removing the extra $(\log n)$ factor present in existing generic bounds. On the other hand, we show that without the i.i.d. assumption, the optimization error can be of order $(\log n)/\sqrt n$. Thus, under the uniformly bounded variance assumption alone, the last iterate of SsGM is suboptimal even in dimension one, resolving negatively an open problem posed in Koren and Segal, COLT, 2020.

</details>

### [99] [Fermi surface change and $d$-wave superconductivity in the square lattice Kondo-Heisenberg model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23799)
*Alexander Nikolaenko,Riccardo Rende,Luciano Loris Viteritti,Subir Sachdev,Ya-Hui Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用神经网络量子态研究二维Kondo-Heisenberg模型，绘制基态相图，发现弱Kondo耦合时呈现反铁磁Néel序（费米面仅计数传导电子），强耦合时呈现重费米液体（费米面计数传导电子和自旋），并在此交叉区域观察到d_{x^2-y^2}超导电性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究二维Kondo-Heisenberg模型中费米体积变化与非传统超导电性是否为其内禀特征，澄清强关联电子体系中Kondo竞争与磁序相互作用下的基态性质。

Method: 采用神经网络量子态（Neural Network Quantum States）作为变分拟设，对正方晶格上非半满传导电子的二维Kondo-Heisenberg模型进行基态求解，系统扫描Kondo耦合与Heisenberg耦合参数空间绘制相图。

Result: (1) 弱Kondo耦合区：反铁磁Néel序，费米面包围面积仅计数传导电子，对Néel序不敏感；(2) 强耦合区：重费米液体，费米面包围面积同时计数传导电子和自旋；(3) 交叉区：d_{x^2-y^2}超导电性，表现为对-对关联中的非对角长程序以及与磁性相共存的配对振幅穹顶。

Conclusion: 费米体积变化和非常规超导电性是二维Kondo-Heisenberg模型的内禀特征，为理解重费米子体系中的量子相变和超导机制提供了关键数值证据。

Abstract: We study the two-dimensional Kondo-Heisenberg model on a square lattice, with the conduction electrons away from half-filling, using neural network quantum states. Mapping the ground-state phase diagram as a function of the Kondo and Heisenberg couplings, we identify (i) at weak Kondo coupling, antiferromagnetic Néel order with a Fermi surface whose enclosed area counts only the conduction electrons and is insensitive to the Néel order, and (ii) at strong coupling, a heavy Fermi liquid with a Fermi surface whose enclosed area counts both the conduction electrons and the spins. In the crossover between these regimes, we find $d_{x^2-y^2}$ superconductivity, evidenced by off-diagonal long-range order in the pair-pair correlations and a pairing-amplitude dome that coexists with the underlying magnetic phase. Our results establish Fermi volume change and unconventional superconductivity as intrinsic features of the two-dimensional Kondo-Heisenberg model.

</details>


### [100] [Domain-wall drift in a ferrimagnet induced by a linearly oscillating in-plane magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23831)
*Murod Mirzhalilov,Jacob Freyermuth*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了铁磁体中畴壁在线性振荡面内磁场下的动力学行为，推导了包含场致惯性项的集体坐标方程，发现结合弱静态面外偏置场可实现畴壁的周期性角度开关整流为有限平均速度，且漂移速度在低频下正比于驱动频率而非幅值，高频下被抑制。微磁模拟验证了理论预测，并表明漂移的大小和方向可通过净自旋密度调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究铁磁体畴壁在线性振荡面内磁场下的动力学行为，特别是场致惯性效应（科里奥利、欧拉、离心力项）如何耦合平移与方位角动力学，并验证是否可实现频率控制的畴壁运动。

Method: 从包含场致动能贡献的铁磁拉格朗日量出发，推导畴壁位置和方位角的集体坐标方程；结合振荡面内场与静态弱面外偏置场，分析周期性角度开关整流为平均漂移速度的机制；通过微磁模拟验证解析预测。

Result: 低频驱动下畴壁角度跟随振荡场，漂移速度正比于驱动频率而非场幅值；高于截止频率后角度无法跟随，平均运动被抑制；漂移大小和方向可通过净自旋密度调节。

Conclusion: 线性振荡面内场可作为铁磁体中频率控制畴壁运动的有效机制，并突显了磁性补偿点附近惯性自旋动力学的重要作用。

Abstract: We investigate ferrimagnetic domain-wall dynamics under linearly oscillating in-plane magnetic fields. Starting from a ferrimagnetic Lagrangian that retains the field-dependent contribution to the kinetic energy, we derive collective-coordinate equations for the domain-wall position and azimuthal angle. This formulation makes explicit the field-induced inertial terms, including Coriolis-, Euler-, and centrifugal-like contributions, and clarifies how they couple translational and azimuthal dynamics. For an oscillating in-plane field supplemented by a weak static out-of-plane bias field, we show that periodic switching of the wall angle can be rectified into a finite average domain-wall velocity. At low drive frequencies, where the wall angle follows the oscillating field, the induced drift is proportional to the drive frequency rather than the field amplitude, while above a cutoff frequency the wall angle can no longer follow the drive and the average motion is suppressed. Micromagnetic simulations confirm the analytical predictions and demonstrate that the magnitude and direction of the drift can be tuned by the net spin density. These results identify linearly oscillating fields as a mechanism for frequency-controlled domain-wall motion in ferrimagnets and highlight the role of inertial spin dynamics near magnetic compensation.

</details>


### [101] [Density-wave order enhances the phonon thermal Hall effect in a trilayer nickelate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24125)
*Qiaochao Xiang,Enkang Zhang,Xiaokang Li,Xiaodong Guo,Mengfei Zhu,Jun Zhao,Guang-Ming Zhang,Liang Li,Zengwei Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了三层镍酸盐La₄Ni₃O₁₀中有限声子热霍尔效应的观测结果，发现在密度波转变温度T*≈140 K以下热霍尔响应显著增强，其能量尺度与磁振子-声子交叉能匹配，表明磁振子-声子杂化是增强热霍尔效应的主要机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐已成为高温超导的重要平台，但晶格自由度在其关联正常态中的作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在常压下测量三层镍酸盐La₄Ni₃O₁₀的热霍尔效应，分析热霍尔电阻率随温度的变化行为。

Result: 观测到有限声子热霍尔效应；热霍尔响应在密度波转变(~140 K)以下显著增强，呈现两个明显的平台；提取的特征能量尺度(~4.1 meV)与磁振子-声子交叉能(~3.2 meV)接近。

Conclusion: 磁振子-声子杂化是增强热霍尔效应的主要机制，为理解镍酸盐中晶格与自旋激发之间的相互作用提供了新见解，对理解其超导性和绝缘热霍尔信号的多种可能起源具有重要意义。

Abstract: Ruddlesden--Popper nickelates have emerged as a promising platform for high-temperature superconductivity, yet the role of lattice degrees of freedom in their correlated normal state remains largely unexplored. Here, we report the observation of a finite phonon thermal Hall effect in the trilayer nickelate La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$ at ambient pressure. Remarkably, the thermal Hall response is strongly enhanced below the density-wave transition at $T^*\approx140$ K, exhibiting two distinct plateaus in the thermal Hall resistivity. The characteristic energy scale extracted from the thermal Hall response ($\sim4.1$ meV) closely matches the magnon--phonon crossing span energy ($\sim3.2$ meV), pointing to magnon--phonon hybridization as the primary mechanism enhancing the thermal Hall effect. These results provide new insight into the interplay between lattice and spin excitations in nickelates, with implications for understanding both their superconductivity and the multiple possible origins of insulating thermal Hall signals.

</details>


### [102] [Fluctuation exchange study on the electron-hole asymmetry of the superconductivity across 1/3 filling in the trilayer Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24126)
*Yushi Yamada,Masataka Kakoi,Masayuki Ochi,Kazuhiko Kuroki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用涨落交换近似研究了三层Hubbard模型，重点关注接近1/3填充的能带情况。当U较小时，超导呈现近似电子-空穴对称行为；当U较大时，超导更倾向于空穴掺杂区域。这种不对称性归因于大U下成键、非键和反键能带的不对称重整化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三层Hubbard模型中，当层间跳跃较大导致成键、非键和反键能带重叠较小时，超导配对机制及其在不同掺杂区域的行为特征。

Method: 采用涨落交换近似（Fluctuation Exchange Approximation, FLEX）方法对三层Hubbard模型进行理论研究，分析不同U值和填充率下的超导特性。

Result: 在U较小时，超导表现出近似的电子-空穴对称行为；而在U较大时，超导在空穴掺杂（从1/3填充移除电子）区域更受青睐，出现明显的电子-空穴不对称性。

Conclusion: 大U下超导的电子-空穴不对称性源于成键、非键和反键能带在强关联作用下的不对称重整化效应。

Abstract: We study within the fluctuation exchange approximation the trilayer Hubbard model where three layers of the Hubbard model are coupled by large interlayer hoppings so that the overlap of the bonding, nonbonding, and antibonding bands is relatively small. We pay special attention to the band fillings close to 1/3, for which the bonding and nonbonding bands as a whole are close to half filling. For relatively small values of the onsite $U$, superconductivity roughly exhibits electron-hole symmetric behavior, as expected for a nearly half-filled two-band system. By contrast, an asymmetry appears when $U$ becomes large, where superconductivity is more favored in the hole-doped regime, i.e., in the regime where electrons are removed from 1/3 filling. We attribute this asymmetry to the asymmetric renormalization of the bonding, nonbonding, and antibonding bands when $U$ is large.

</details>


### [103] [Magnetoelectric flat band induced by a $\sqrt{3}\times\sqrt{3}$ charge density wave in monolayer CrSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24494)
*Pablo Savino,M. del Carmen Fuente Santiago,Jan Phillips,Javier Corral Sertal,Adolfo O. Fumega,Santiago Blanco-Canosa,Victor Pardo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算研究了CrSe₂中极性的√3×√3电荷密度波（CDW）相的电子和磁性质，揭示了CDW畸变在范德华平面外引入的极性畸变与自旋极化Cr d态耦合，在费米能级处产生了一个显著平坦的电子能带。通过分析结构重构、轨道杂化、晶体场劈裂、自旋轨道耦合和电子关联，提供了该平带的微观理解。计算表明，由于CDW畸变的极性本质，电场可以作为外部开关诱导CDW相，从而提供了一种操控系统中强关联的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解CrSe₂中极性CDW相的电子和磁性质，特别是平带形成的微观机制，以及探索利用电场操控强关联相的可能性。

Method: 采用第一性原理（ab initio）计算方法，分析结构重构、轨道杂化、晶体场劈裂、自旋轨道耦合和电子关联对电子结构的影响。

Result: 极性CDW畸变与自旋极化Cr d态耦合，在费米能级处产生了一个异常平坦的电子能带；电场可作为外部开关诱导CDW相。

Conclusion: CDW畸变的极性特性使得电场能够作为一种外部手段来诱导和操控CDW相，为调控系统中的强关联电子态提供了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate the electronic and magnetic properties of the polar $\sqrt{3}\times\sqrt{3}$ charge-density-wave (CDW) phase of CrSe$_2$ using ab initio calculations. The CDW introduces a polar distortion out of the van der Waals plane that couples to the spin-polarized Cr d states resulting in a remarkably flat electronic band exactly at the Fermi level. We provide a microscopic understanding of the origin of the flat band by analyzing in detail the structural reconstruction, the effects of orbital hybridization, crystal-field splittings, spin-orbit coupling and electronic correlations. Our calculations show that due to the polar nature of the CDW distortion, an electric field can act as an external switch to induce the CDW phase, providing a way to manipulate strong correlations in the system.

</details>


### [104] [On Degeneracies of Density, Magnetic, and Pairing Responses: How Competing Orders Echo Underlying Symmetries in the Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24755)
*Michael Meixner,Herbert Eßl,Matthias Reitner,Alessandro Toschi,Thomas Schäfer*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用赝自旋对称性，严格推导了无阻挫二方格子Hubbard模型中电荷、自旋和配对 susceptibilities 之间的互易关系，并通过双粒子数值模拟展示了在金属-绝缘体转变附近d波配对/d-密度波（环电流）不稳定性的同时出现，揭示了几何阻挫如何逐渐打破这种简并性从而有利于超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联电子系统中往往存在竞争甚至交织的序倾向，暗示多电子能量高度接近或简并。虽然简并直接源于问题的对称性，但其对不同响应函数的多方面影响及相互关系仍难以捉摸。本文旨在严格建立这一主题的基础。

Method: 利用无阻挫二方格子Hubbard模型的赝自旋对称性，解析推导了电荷、自旋和配对 susceptibilities 在一般空间调制下的互易关系；通过双粒子数值模拟展示了二维情况下金属-绝缘体转变附近的不稳定性。

Result: 发现了在金属-绝缘体转变附近同时出现d波配对/d-密度波（环电流）不稳定性，该不稳定性由短程自旋涨落驱动；几何阻挫会逐渐打破这种简并性，从而有利于超导序。

Conclusion: 本研究为揭示关联电子系统中的对称性关系提供了通用工具，并建立了对电荷/环电流、配对和自旋序交织本质的统一视角。

Abstract: Strongly correlated electron systems often display competing or even intertwined ordering tendencies, hinting to extremely close or degenerate many-electron energies. While degeneracies are directly rooted in the underlying symmetries of the problem under investigation, their multifaceted effects on different response functions and their mutual relations often remain elusive. Here we put this subject on a rigorous basis by investigating the degeneracies of charge, spin, and pairing susceptibilities for the unfrustrated, bipartite Hubbard model. Exploiting its pseudospin symmetry, we analytically derive the mutual relations between these response functions for generic spatial modulations, highly relevant, e.g., for the competition of stripe and superconducting orders. By means of two-particle numerical simulations we demonstrate the occurrence of a simultaneous $d$-wave pairing/$d$-density wave (loop current) instability in the vicinity of the metal-insulator transition, driven by short-ranged spin fluctuations for the two-dimensional case. We show how this degeneracy is gradually lifted by geometrical frustration, which favors superconductivity. Our study provides a general tool for revealing symmetry relations in correlated electron systems and establishes a unifying perspective on the nature of their intermingled charge/loop current, pairing, and spin orders.

</details>


### [105] [The Interplay of Thermal Melting and Pump Driven Melting of Charge Order: A Two-Temperature Study of the Holstein Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24800)
*Debraj Bose,Sankha Subhra Bakshi,Pinaki Majumdar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了强光激发后电荷有序态的熔化和恢复动力学，通过将电子视为准平衡态（具有随时间变化的电子温度$T_{\rm el}(t)$），系统声子耦合到热浴（温度$T_{\rm bath}$），并考虑Holstein电子-声子相互作用，建立了以$T_{\rm el}$和$T_{\rm bath}$为参数的相图，分析了序参量动力学、静态性质和准稳态激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡态下电子-声子耦合驱动的电荷有序已被充分理解，但泵浦-探测实验提出了新问题：强光激发后这种有序如何熔化和恢复？

Method: 采用近似方案，将泵浦诱导的电子激发建模为缓慢时变的“电子温度”$T_{\rm el}(t)$，表征准平衡电子态；系统声子耦合到温度为$T_{\rm bath}$的热浴；考虑Holstein电子-声子相互作用。在不同$T_{\rm el}$和$T_{\rm bath}$组合下求解问题。

Result: 建立了以$T_{\rm bath}$和$T_{\rm el}$为参数的“相图”，揭示了序参量动力学、长时间“准稳态”的静态性质和激发行为。

Conclusion: 该计算框架通过电子温度近似，为理解光激发下电荷有序态的熔化和恢复动力学提供了有效途径，并揭示了闭系统（能量守恒）与开系统（存在涨落与耗散）之间的关键差异。

Abstract: Charge order driven by electron-phonon coupling is well understood at equilibrium but pump-probe experiments raise a new question: how does this order melt and recover after strong photoexcitation? A pump pulse promotes carriers across the charge-order gap and creates a nonequilibrium high-energy electronic population. In a closed system the subsequent dynamics is constrained by energy conservation. In an `open system' - where the system is coupled to a thermal bath at some temperature $T_{\rm bath}$ - there are new fluctuation and dissipation processes at play. One can attempt a computational scheme that incorporates coupling of electrons to a laser pump, the coupling of system phonons to a thermal bath, and the Holstein interaction that couples electrons and phonons. We attempt an approximation where the pump induced electronic excitations are modeled by a slowly time varying `electron temperature', $T_{\rm el}(t)$, indicative of a quasi-equilibrium electronic state. We solve the problem for different combinations of $T_{\rm el}$ and $T_{\rm bath}$, probing the order parameter dynamics, the static properties and excitations in the long time `quasi steady state', and establish a `phase diagram' in terms of bath temperature and electron temperature.

</details>


### [106] [Introduction to matrix-product states and tensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24803)
*Grégoire Misguich*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统介绍了基于张量网络的量子多体物理方法，重点阐述矩阵乘积态（MPS）的核心概念、算法及其在开放量子系统中的应用，并配有Julia代码示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为2026年Les Houches暑期学校提供关于张量网络方法在量子多体物理及开放量子系统中应用的教学材料，帮助研究人员掌握从MPS到PEPS以及混合态张量网络表示的基础理论与实用算法。

Method: 通过图文符号（图形记号）、虚拟指标、键维度、规范自由度、规范形式、QR分解和奇异值分解等基本概念搭建张量网络语言；在此基础上介绍MPS收缩、关联函数计算、矩阵乘积算符（MPO）、DMRG和时间演化方法；随后推广到投影纠缠对态（PEPS）及其近似收缩；最后扩展到混合态、量子信道和Lindblad动力学的张量网络表示。

Result: 完整构建了从基础张量网络符号到核心算法（DMRG、时间演化、PEPS收缩）再到开放量子系统应用的理论框架，并提供基于ITensor、ITensorMPS和TensorMixedStates的Julia代码示例作为实践支撑。

Conclusion: 张量网络方法（特别是MPS）是处理量子多体问题及开放量子系统的有效工具，其计算效率由纠缠结构决定；PEPS作为高维推广可处理更复杂的系统，而混合态表示则拓展了该方法在热态和开放系统中的应用场景。

Abstract: These notes provide an introduction to tensor-network methods in quantum many-body physics, with an emphasis on matrix-product states (MPS). They develop the basic tensor-network language, including graphical notation, virtual indices, bond dimensions, gauge freedom, canonical forms, QR and singular-value decompositions, and the role of entanglement in controlling the efficiency of the representation. The main MPS algorithms are then introduced, including contractions, correlation functions, matrix-product operators, DMRG, and time-evolution methods. The notes also briefly discuss projected entangled-pair states (PEPS) as a higher-dimensional generalization of MPS, together with the basic ideas behind approximate PEPS contraction. Finally, tensor-network representations of mixed states, quantum channels, and Lindblad dynamics are presented, with applications to thermal states and open quantum systems. The presentation is accompanied by short Julia code examples based on ITensor, ITensorMPS, and TensorMixedStates. These notes were written for the 9th Les Houches Summer School on Computational Physics: Open Quantum Systems, held in June 2026.

</details>


### [107] [THz-induced phonon mode mixing and collective dynamics in a polar nanolattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24804)
*Elizabeth Skoropata,Peter M. Derlet,Hiroki Ueda,Roman Mankowsky,Mathias Sander,Henrik T. Lemke,Rafael T. Winkler,Yunpei Deng,Milan Radovic,Matteo Savoini,Biaolong Liu,Martina Basini,Vladimir Ovuka,Steven L. Johnson,Marta D. Rossell,Urs Staub*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过时间分辨X射线衍射和分子动力学模拟，研究了SrTiO₃薄膜中纳米有序界面位错网络在THz激发下的动力学响应，发现跨尺度对称性破缺可产生动态电极化和涡旋状集体模式，为通过外延控制实空间拓扑来探索动态功能特性开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越传统晶胞尺度（如应变、掺杂、光等）对称性控制方法，探索更高阶结构（如纳米尺度的有序界面位错网络）对声子散射和干涉效应的调控潜力，以实现新的动态功能特性。

Method: 采用2D和3D时间分辨X射线衍射探测SrTiO₃薄膜中纳米有序界面位错网络在THz激发下的动力学，并结合经典分子动力学模拟进行理论验证。

Result: 发现跨尺度（从晶胞到纳米结构）对称性破缺能有效产生动态电极化，并控制声子混合，从而产生此前未报道的THz波段圆环状涡旋位移集体模式。

Conclusion: 这项工作建立了一种通过外延控制实空间拓扑结构来探索动态功能特性的新范式，可推广至磁性、电性和铁电系统。

Abstract: Manipulating phonons through symmetry is a fundamental approach to alter the dynamic responses of materials. Most often new phases are sought through control of the unit-cell (e.g. via strain, doping, light, etc.). By comparison, there is vast potential to look beyond the unit-cell into higher-order architectures to control wave scattering and interference effects that remains less explored. We describe the THz-induced dynamics of an SrTiO$_{3}$ thin film with a nanoscale ordered interfacial dislocation network probed with 2- and 3-dimensional time-resolved x-ray diffraction and classical molecular dynamics simulations. We find that symmetry breaking at all scales is an effective approach to create a dynamical electric polarization and to control phonon mixing that generates previously unreported collective modes in the THz regime with circular vortex-like displacements. This work opens a new pathway to explore dynamical functional properties that can be extended to magnetic, electric, and ferroelectric systems by controlling the real-space topology via epitaxy.

</details>


### [108] [Topological lattice gauge theory enriched by non-invertible symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28688)
*Lea E. Bottini,Clement Delcamp,Edmund Heng,Campbell K. McLauchlan,Dominic J. Williamson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文以有限群拓扑格点规范理论（量子双模型）为视角，研究非可逆对称性如何丰富拓扑序。通过在量子双模型中凝聚任意荷代数，证明局域激发的范畴形成超群分次的扩展。超群中的每个元素对应的畴壁对局域激发具有非可逆作用，该作用与幺半结构均与超群分次兼容。范畴作用编码为局域激发范畴上的Hopf monad，规范非可逆对称性等价于计算该monad的模范畴。最后概述该框架如何自然推广到一般弦网模型中的凝聚理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可逆对称性丰富拓扑序已有严格公理化（G-交叉辫子融合范畴），但对于非可逆对称性丰富拓扑序缺乏相应的数学框架。本文旨在建立这一推广的基础。

Method: 利用量子双模型（有限群拓扑格点规范理论）作为实验平台，通过凝聚任意荷代数构造新理论；分析局域激发范畴的超群分次结构；研究畴壁的非可逆作用；引入Hopf monad编码范畴作用；通过模范畴构造实现规范变换。

Result: 局域激发范畴是超群分次的；超群元素对应的畴壁以非可逆方式作用于局域激发；该作用与幺半结构兼容；非可逆对称性的规范由Hopf monad的模范畴给出。

Conclusion: 建立了非可逆对称性丰富拓扑序的数学基础框架，并指出该框架可推广至任意弦网模型中的代数凝聚情形。

Abstract: We use finite group topological lattice gauge theory, also known as the quantum double model, as a lens to explore a notion of topological order enriched by a non-invertible symmetry. For invertible symmetry enriched topological order, there is an established axiomatisation in terms of a G-crossed braided fusion category. We lay the foundations for a generalisation of this notion. By condensing an arbitrary algebra of charges in a quantum double model, we demonstrate that the category of localised excitations in the resulting theory forms a hypergroup-graded extension of the category of deconfined excitations. For every element in the hypergroup, the associated domain wall acts in a typically non-invertible way on these localised excitations. Both this action and the monoidal structure are compatible with the hypergroup grading. The actual categorical action is encoded in a Hopf monad on the category of localised excitations, and gauging the non-invertible symmetry amounts to computing the category of modules over this Hopf monad. Finally, we outline how this framework naturally extends to theories obtained by condensing algebras in a generic string-net model.

</details>

### [109] [Modern tidal interaction models for rapid binary population synthesis: II. Binary black hole formation, mergers, and spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23773)
*Veome Kapil,Ilya Mandel,Jeff Riley,Evgeni Grishin,Jim Fuller,Emanuele Berti*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用COMPAS代码中的自洽潮汐耗散模型，预测孤立双星演化形成的双黑洞并合率及有效自旋分布。结果表明：第一代黑洞大多零自旋（除化学均匀演化外），第二代黑洞自旋显著依赖于潮汐耗散效率和质量传输历史；高有效自旋双星因轨道间距小、并合时间短，主要在高红移处并合，当前引力波探测器难以探测。当前探测器探测到的自旋分布严重偏向低有效自旋（1/3系统χ_eff<0.05，仅~3%>0.5），但随着探测器对高红移源灵敏度提升，χ_eff>0.5的系统比例可增至~15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进双黑洞自旋分布的预测模型，理解潮汐耗散在孤立双星演化中的关键作用，并解释当前引力波观测中自旋分布偏低的成因。

Method: 使用快速双星星族合成代码COMPAS，引入新的自洽潮汐耗散实现模型，模拟孤立双星演化路径，预测双黑洞的并合率与有效自旋分布。

Result: ①第一代BH大多零自旋（除化学均匀演化外）；②第二代BH自旋强烈依赖于潮汐耗散效率和质量传输历史，且不一定在超新星前同步；③高χ_eff系统轨道间距小、并合时间短，主要在高红移并合，当前探测器难以探测；④当前探测器探测到的自旋分布偏向低χ_eff（33%<0.05，仅3%>0.5）；⑤未来探测器灵敏度提升后，χ_eff>0.5比例可达15%。

Conclusion: 孤立双星演化形成的双黑洞自旋分布存在显著的观测选择性效应，当前探测器只能探测到低有效自旋的样本，但随着下一代探测器对高红移源的探测能力增强，高自旋双黑洞的比例将显著增加。

Abstract: We present predictions for the merger rates and effective spin ($χ_{\rm eff}$) distribution of binary black holes (BBHs) from isolated binary evolution, using a new self-consistent tidal dissipation implementation in the rapid binary population synthesis code COMPAS. Most of the first-born black holes (BHs) in our simulated merging BBHs are formed with zero spins, with the exception of BBHs formed from chemically homogeneous evolution. The spins of the second-born BHs with the new model depend significantly on the efficiency of tidal dissipation and mass transfer history, and crucially, are not always consistent with pre-supernova synchronization. High-$χ_{\rm eff}$ binaries preferentially merge at high redshift due to smaller binary separations at BBH formation and shorter coalescence times, thus rendering them largely inaccessible to current gravitational wave (GW) detectors. We expect the intrinsic spin distribution of merging BBHs formed from isolated evolution to be strongly biased toward low $χ_{\rm eff}$ with current detectors, with a third of systems having $χ_{\rm eff} < 0.05$ and only $\sim 3\%$ with $χ_{\rm eff}>0.5$. However, $χ_{\rm eff}$ will increase as GW detectors become sensitive to higher redshift sources, with up to $\sim 15\%$ of systems having $χ_{\rm eff}>0.5$.

</details>


### [110] [Signatures of $^{56}$Ni Mixing and Neutron-rich Ejecta in Supernovae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23780)
*Nikhil Sarin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过多壳层模型系统研究了超新星和坍缩星中放射性物质混合（⁵⁶Ni混合及r-process物质）对光变曲线的影响。结果表明，向外混合⁵⁶Ni会导致光变曲线更快更亮地上升，并改变晚期衰减行为；单区模型拟合混合光变曲线会得到偏倚的物理参数（低估抛射质量、高估镍质量分数）。对于坍缩星中的r-process物质，其信号特征并非总是近红外超，而取决于镍背景、径向位置、角分布和观测视角。盘风模型显示，快速上升的on-axis GRB-SNe只有在r-process物质达到赤道面以上≥30°纬度时才具有约束力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超新星光变曲线解释多采用单区放射性衰变模型，忽略了放射性物质分布这一关键变量对参数推断和模型解释的影响。本文旨在系统探究⁵⁶Ni混合和r-process物质分布对光变曲线的影响，并评估单区模型拟合混合光变曲线时的参数偏差。

Method: 采用多壳层模型（multi-shell model）模拟超新星和坍缩星中放射性物质的分布效应。通过改变⁵⁶Ni的径向分布研究混合对光变曲线上升、峰值和尾部的影响；通过不同几何构型和观测视角模拟坍缩星中r-process物质的信号特征。同时对比单区模型与多壳层模型的拟合结果，分析参数偏差。

Result: ① 向外混合⁵⁶Ni减少覆盖扩散柱，产生更快更亮的光变曲线上升，且改变尾部因局部伽马射线泄漏；② 单区模型拟合混合光变曲线会低估抛射质量M_ej、高估镍质量分数f_Ni，而总镍质量M_Ni相对稳定；若视不透明度为自由参数，完全混合情况恢复为κ^fit/κ^input≈0.24；③ r-process信号并非总是近红外超，依赖于镍背景、径向位置、角分布和视角；④ 盘风模型中，赤道面约束的r-process物质不产生明显信号，只有当纬度≥30°时才具有可探测性。

Conclusion: 放射性物质的分布是解释超新星光变曲线的关键变量，忽略它将导致系统性的参数偏差（如爆炸能量和前身星映射的误判）。快速明亮的上升不能直接作为低抛射质量或引擎驱动的证据。r-process信号的探测需考虑复杂的几何和观测效应，赤道约束的盘风模型对快速上升的on-axis GRB-SNe不构成有效约束。

Abstract: Supernova lightcurves are often interpreted with one-zone radioactive-decay models that ignore a key variable that can affect interpretation and inferred parameters: the distribution of radioactive material. Using a multi-shell model, we explore the impact of $^{56}$Ni mixing in supernovae and r-process material in collapsars. Moving $^{56}$Ni outward reduces the overlying diffusion column, producing faster and brighter rises at fixed $M_{\rm Ni}$, $M_{\rm ej}$, and $E_{\rm k}$, and changes the tail through local gamma-ray leakage. A fast, bright rise is not, by itself, evidence for low ejecta mass or a requirement for engine power, with significant overlap between highly mixed and engine-powered lightcurves. One-zone fits to mixed bolometric light curves produce visually good fits but biased parameters. At fixed opacity, outward mixing is absorbed mainly by low inferred $M_{\rm ej}$ and high inferred $f_{\rm Ni}$, while $M_{\rm Ni}$ remains stable. If opacity is free, the fully mixed case is recovered with $κ^{\rm fit}/κ^{\rm input}\simeq0.24$. These shifts affect inferred explosion energies and progenitor mappings, and amplified in photometric fits. Exploring collapsar r-process enrichment, we find that the signature is not always a NIR excess and depends sensitively on the nickel-powered background, radial placement, angular distribution, and viewing angle of neutron-rich ejecta. In our setup, spherical models often show optical suppression and delayed colour evolution. Our disk-wind models suggest that fast-rising on-axis GRB-SNe are poor r-process targets for equatorially confined neutron-rich winds, and become constraining only if the r-process material reaches latitudes $\gtrsim 30^\circ$ from the equatorial plane.

</details>

James,Saurabh W. Jha,Charles D. Kilpatrick,Lindsey A. Kwok,Gavin P. Lamb,Amanda R. Lopes,Michael Lundquist,Clara E. Martinez-Vasquez,Thomas Matheson,Curtis McCully,Maryam Modjaz,Gregory S. H. Paek,Antonella Palmese,Avi Patel,Joanne L. Pledger,Aravind P. Ravi,Jeonghee Rho,Krisztian Sarneczky,Manisha Shrestha,Richard Smith,Analia V. Smith Castelli,Monika Soraisam,Jay Strader,Francisco Valdes,Sergiy Vasylyev,V. Ashley Villar,A. Katherina Vivas,Lifan Wang,Samuel D. Wyatt,Kathryn Wynn,WeiKang Zheng*
