TL;DR: DECam Shadow Survey通过同步观测LSST的星系团场，以高频率发现极早期超新星及瞬变现象。基于该调查，论文报告了室女座星系NGC4532中AT2017des的LBV爆发活动，其光度超过正常LBV，且爆发峰值随时间逐渐增亮，可能预示最终爆炸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大化LSST的科学潜力，通过同步观测（shadowing）实现对局部星系团场的高频率监测，以发现极早期超新星、前兆爆发及其他罕见瞬变现象。

Method: 利用DECam对LSST观测的星系团场进行同步跟踪（Shadow Survey），实现夜间高频次监测；结合多台站长期测光数据及光谱时间序列，对候选体进行证认和分析。

Result: AT2017des表现出短时标（约10天）的光变起伏，峰值绝对星等约Mr=-12.5等，高于正常LBV；其爆发峰值亮度随时间逐渐增加，2026年峰值流量比2023年高约5倍，平均增亮速率约0.05 mag/yr。

Conclusion: AT2017des的爆发活动正逐渐增强，可能正处于“爬坡”阶段，预示其最终可能发生终爆（terminal explosion）。

Abstract: The Legacy Survey of Space and Time (LSST) will start in late-summer 2026, revolutionizing transient astronomy. Here, we present the Dark Energy Camera (DECam) Shadow Survey, which is designed to maximize the science potential of LSST by shadowing LSST observations of local galaxy-cluster fields, producing a nightly cadence of these fields. The Shadow Survey will discover extremely young supernovae (SNe), SN precursors, as well as other explosive transients and exotic phenomena, helping to characterize such transients at unprecedented cadence and depth when combined with LSST. We describe our workflow, pipeline, public data releases, and candidate vetting. As an early result of Shadow, we present the fitful luminous blue variable (LBV) eruptions of AT2017des in the Virgo-Cluster galaxy NGC4532. AT2017des has short-timescale variability (of order 10 days), peaking at around $M_r=-12.5$mag, brighter than normal LBVs, and similar to the more extreme flaring of hot LBVs/SN impostors such as SN2000ch, AT2016blu, and the precursor activity of SN2009ip. Our spectral time-series reveals features typical of these hot LBVs and SN impostors/precursors. Combining our data with long-baseline photometry from additional observatories, we find that the peaks of the outbursts of AT2017des are getting brighter over time, with 2026 peak fluxes being up to 5 times greater than in 2023 and an average brightening of $\sim0.05$ mag yr$^{-1}$. The peaks of AT2017des are more luminous than those of most other LBVs, only being fainter than bright precursors such as SN2009ip, and extreme SN impostors such as AT2016blu. AT2017des may therefore be ``ramping up'' to a terminal explosion.

</details>


### [112] [A Collapsar-Disk Origin for GW190814](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23786)
*Vishal Baibhav,Brian D. Metzger,Lam Hui*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出GW190814引力波事件源于坍缩星盘碎片与中心黑洞的合并，并发现SN2019npv Ib型超新星作为其电磁前驱体候选，为这一特殊事件提供了新的形成通道解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW190814事件中23倍太阳质量黑洞与2.6倍太阳质量致密天体的极端质量比合并难以通过标准孤立双星或动力学形成渠道解释，需要新的形成机制。

Method: 利用中微子冷却的坍缩星盘引力不稳定碎裂产生中子星或低质量黑洞的理论模型，分析盘碎片与中心黑洞的相互作用及合并时标；通过搜索GW190814误差体积内的瞬变事件识别电磁对应体候选。

Result: 识别出SN2019npv Ib型超新星候选者，其发生在GW190814置信体积内、合并前约60天；三体相互作用可将致密天体激发到宽轨道，产生数周至数月的合并延迟。将SN2019npv作为宿主给出H_0=70.5(+9.2,-6.4) km/s/Mpc。

Conclusion: GW190814可能起源于坍缩星盘碎片合并，该通道预测引力波事件前有剥离包层超新星伴随；未来此类延迟合并可产生可探测的潮汐瓦解或激波发射。

Abstract: GW190814 was a remarkable gravitational-wave (GW) event: a merger between a 23 solar-mass black hole (BH) and a 2.6 solar-mass compact object, with an extreme mass ratio that is difficult to reproduce through standard isolated-binary or dynamical formation channels. Recent work has shown that neutrino-cooled collapsar disks can become gravitationally unstable and fragment, producing neutron stars (NSs) or low-mass BHs in orbit around the newly formed central BH. These fragments may subsequently interact, scatter, merge with one another, or inspiral into the central remnant. We propose that GW190814 originated from such a collapsar-disk fragment merging with the central BH. A key prediction of this scenario is a temporal association with a stripped-envelope supernova preceding the GW event, and we identify the Type Ib supernova candidate SN2019npv, which occurred inside the GW190814 credible volume approximately 60 days before coalescence, as a possible electromagnetic precursor. Although this delay is too long for a conventional kilonova counterpart, we show that three-body interactions among disk fragments can excite some compact objects to wide orbits and naturally produce merger delays of weeks to months. While GW190814 itself was not expected to produce detectable tidal-disruption-powered emission, future delayed mergers in this channel could generate luminous transients through either reprocessed kilonova heating or shocks driven as merger ejecta collide with the preceding supernova ejecta. Finally, treating SN2019npv as the host makes GW190814 a bright standard siren and yields H_0 = 70.5 (+9.2, -6.4) km/s/Mpc.

</details>


### [113] [Composition of Radiation-Driven Winds from Type I X-ray Bursts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23818)
*Jason S. Pero,Nevin N. Weinberg*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用MESA恒星演化代码模拟光球半径膨胀（PRE）X射线暴，发现风物质中的元素丰度敏感依赖于点火深度和对流处理方式（半对流混合效率、对流边界定义）。点火深度≥5×10^8 g cm⁻²时，抛射物富含中等到铁峰元素，这为通过PRE暴吸收特征探测X射线暴和中子星性质提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NICER对PRE X射线暴的观测揭示了中间质量元素的吸收特征，这些特征可能源于辐射驱动风抛射的新鲜核合成灰烬，为研究X射线暴和中子星性质提供了新探针。然而，理论模拟尚不完善，需要系统研究不同物理参数（点火深度、对流处理）对风物质组成的影响。

Method: 使用MESA恒星演化代码，从吸积阶段到流体动力学风阶段对PRE暴进行全程模拟。考虑了纯氦和氢/氦混合吸积两种情形，覆盖不同点火深度，并测试了多种对流方案（包括半对流混合效率和对流边界定义的不同处理方式）。

Result: 风物质丰度对点火深度和对流处理（半对流混合效率、对流边界定义）均高度敏感。当点火深度≥5×10^8 g cm⁻²时，抛射物中富含核合成灰烬，其成分范围从中间质量元素到铁峰元素，具体取决于点火深度、吸积组成和对流处理方式。

Conclusion: PRE暴抛射物的元素组成是点火深度和微物理过程（对流）的复杂函数。这为解释NICER观测到的吸收特征提供了理论基础，同时也表明需要更精细的对流模型才能从观测中可靠反演中子星性质。

Abstract: Recent NICER observations of photospheric radius expansion (PRE) X-ray bursts reveal absorption features consistent with photospheres enriched in intermediate-mass elements. These features may arise from radiation-driven winds that eject freshly synthesized nuclear ashes, offering a new probe of X-ray bursts and neutron star properties. Motivated by these observations, we use the MESA stellar evolution code to simulate PRE bursts from accretion through the hydrodynamic wind phase. We model a range of ignition depths for both pure helium and mixed hydrogen/helium accretion and explore several prescriptions for convection during burst rise. We find that the wind abundances depend sensitively on both ignition depth and convective treatment, including the efficiency of semiconvective mixing and the prescription used to define convective boundaries. Bursts igniting at column depths greater than or equal to 5 x 10^8 g cm^-2 produce ash-enriched winds, with ejecta ranging from intermediate-mass to iron-peak elements depending on ignition depth, accretion composition, and the treatment of convection.

</details>


### [114] [Indication for Decreasing Dispersion Measure in the Population of Repeating Fast Radio Bursts and Connection to Young Supernova Remnant Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23903)
*Xiang-han Cui,Cheng-min Zhang,Di Li,Pei Wang,Erbil Gügercinoğlu,Joeri van Leeuwen,Yi-dan Wang,Chao-wei Tsai,Xiang-lei Chen,Wen-qi Ma,Habtamu Menberu Tedila*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用CHIME探测到的19个重复快速射电暴（FRB）的长期色散量（DM）演化数据，发现7个源有显著DM演化（5个下降、2个上升），并通过二项式检验证明下降趋势在统计上更显著（p=0.033），支持了局部电子密度随时间递减的假设（如超新星遗迹膨胀模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复FRB是研究其物理本质的重要样本，但对其长期参数演化（尤其是DM变化）的统计特征尚不明确，需要系统分析以区分不同物理模型。

Method: 从CHIME监测的63个重复FRB中选出19个爆发次数>10的源，分析其DM长期演化趋势；对7个有显著演变的源进行二项式检验（零假设：上升/下降概率相等）；结合文献数据扩大样本后再次检验；在超新星遗迹膨胀模型框架下估算不同抛射质量对DM的贡献。

Result: 19个源中7个有显著DM演化（黄金样本），其中5个下降、2个上升；黄金样本二项式检验p=0.227，但结合文献数据后组合样本p=0.033，表明下降趋势在重复FRB总体中更常见；超新星遗迹膨胀模型可定性地解释DM下降趋势。

Conclusion: 重复FRB的DM演化以下降趋势为主，支持其局部环境电子密度随时间递减（如超新星遗迹膨胀）的物理图像。

Abstract: Fast Radio Bursts (FRBs) are millisecond-duration, highly energetic radio transients of uncertain origin. Repeating FRBs provide an excellent population for investigating their nature, particularly through studies of parameter evolution. Out of the 63 repeaters monitored by CHIME, we select the 19 sources with more than ten detected bursts, and examine their long-term dispersion measure (DM) evolution. Seven sources show statistically significant DM evolution and are classified as the golden sample. Of these, five exhibit a decreasing DM trend and two shows an increasing trend. We then perform a binomial test under the null hypothesis that decreasing and increasing DM variation trends have equal probabilities. The current combined sample, including our golden sample and additional repeaters with reported DM change rate from the literature, gives a p-value of 0.033, supporting that decreasing DM trends are more common in the repeating FRB population. This statistical result is consistent with scenarios in that the local electron density around repeaters generally decreases with time, for example due to expansion of a young supernova remnant (SNR). Finally, within the SNR expansion model, we provide an illustrative estimate of the SNR contributions to the DM for different ejecta masses.

</details>


### [115] [Deep Newtonian Afterglows: Theoretical Light Curves for Quasi-spherical Outflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23904)
*Nissim Fraija,Boris Betancourt-Kamenetskaia,Antonio Galván,Maria G. Dainotti*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了准球形外流在深牛顿阶段与分层介质相互作用产生的伽马射线暴晚期余辉，开发了含能量注入的同步辐射解析框架，并应用于GRB 171205A及短暴样本的观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准球形亚相对论性抛射物在致密星并合或核心坍缩爆炸中自然形成速度结构，长期中心引擎的能量注入（自转减慢和/或回落吸积）会显著改变余辉演化，但现有模型对深牛顿阶段的系统描述不足，需要建立解析框架来理解晚期多波段观测。

Method: 建立适用于分层介质中减速抛射物组分在能量注入下的同步辐射解析模型；推导深牛顿体制的多波段光变曲线和闭合关系；将模型应用于GRB 171205A的晚期观测，并结合短暴样本的多年射电上限约束准球形外流物理性质。

Result: 成功建立包含能量注入和分层介质的深牛顿阶段余辉解析框架；对GRB 171205A实现多波段拟合；利用短暴关联千新星候选体及长射电上限样本，有效约束了准球形外流的动力学、能量学及能量注入历史。

Conclusion: 晚期观测能够对GRB progenitors产生的亚相对论准球形外流的动力学、能量学和能量注入历史提供有意义的约束。

Abstract: We investigate late-time gamma-ray burst (GRB) afterglows produced by quasi-spherical outflows propagating into a stratified circumburst medium during the deep Newtonian phase. Sub-relativistic ejecta generated in compact binary mergers or core-collapse explosions naturally develop velocity structures, while additional energy injection from a long-lived central engine, through spin-down luminosity and/or fallback accretion, can substantially modify the afterglow evolution. We develop an analytical framework for synchrotron emission from decelerated ejecta components undergoing energy injection in a stratified environment. The model provides multiwavelength light curves and corresponding closure relations for the deep Newtonian regime. We apply this framework to the late-time multiwavelength observations of GRB 171205A. In addition, we constrain the physical properties of quasi-spherical outflows using observations of short GRBs associated with kilonova candidates, together with long-term radio upper limits obtained years after the burst in a broader GRB sample. Our results show that late-time observations can place meaningful constraints on the dynamics, energetics, and energy-injection history of sub-relativistic quasi-spherical outflows from GRB progenitors.

</details>


### [116] [Detection of relativistic orbital deformation from improved timing of PSR J1757$-$1854](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23926)
*Jaikhomba Singha,Vivek Venkatraman Krishnan,Marisa Geyer,Victoria Blackmon,Paulo Freire,Norbert Wex,Maura McLaughlin,Michael Kramer,Amanda Weltman,David Champion,Matthew Bailes,Sarah Buchner,Fernando Camilo,Andrea Possenti,Maciej Serylak*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过对PSR~J1757$-$1854双中子星系统的高精度计时观测，首次探测到相对论角形变参数δ_θ，并利用该参数约束了系统的自旋-轨道几何结构，排除了四种可能解中的两种。同时评估了高阶效应对近星点进动和总系统质量测量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSR~J1757$-$1854是一个高度相对论性的双中子星系统，具有极大的引力波亮度和最快的轨道衰减率之一，是检验强场引力的理想实验室。此前对其后开普勒参数的测量精度有限，且δ_θ尚未被探测到。

Method: 结合Murriyang望远镜、Green Bank望远镜的存档数据与MeerKAT射电望远镜的高灵敏度新观测数据，进行高精度计时分析。

Result: 后开普勒参数精度提升约2倍以上；首次探测到δ_θ，使其成为第三个测量到该参数的DNS系统（仅需9年）；利用δ_θ排除四种自旋-轨道几何解中的两种；发现二阶后牛顿修正和Lense-Thirring项对总系统质量有可测量的系统效应；轨道周期导数与GR引力波阻尼预言一致。

Conclusion: PSR~J1757$-$1854的高精度计时结果与广义相对论一致，验证了该系统的强场引力检验能力，并展示了新观测设施（MeerKAT）对提升测量精度的关键作用。

Abstract: PSR~J1757$-$1854, a 21.5\,ms pulsar, is a highly relativistic double neutron star (DNS) system in a tight eccentric ($e = 0.61$) 4.4\,hr orbit. With extremely large gravitational wave luminosity and one of the fastest orbital decay rates of any known DNS system, it is ideal for testing general relativity (GR) in the strong-field regime. Here we present results from a high-precision timing campaign combining archival data from the Murriyang telescope and Green Bank Telescope (GBT) with new high-sensitivity observations from the MeerKAT radio telescope and additional observations from the GBT. The extended baseline and superior sensitivity of MeerKAT have yielded substantial improvements to previously measured post-Keplerian parameters by a factor of around $\sim2$ or more. We report the first detection of the relativistic angular deformation, $δ_θ$ in this system, making PSR~J1757$-$1854 only the third DNS system for which $δ_θ$ has been measured, achieved here in just 9 yrs compared to the decades of timing required for both the double pulsar and the Hulse-Taylor binary. We demonstrate how $δ_θ$ can be used to constrain the spin-orbit geometry of the system, ruling out two of the four geometric solutions previously identified, while remaining consistent with GR. We also evaluate higher-order contributions to the periastron advance $\dotω$, including the second post-Newtonian correction and the Lense-Thirring term, and show that these have a measurable systematic effect on the inferred total system mass. The observed orbital period derivative, $\dot{P}_\mathrm{b}$ remains consistent with the GR prediction for gravitational-wave damping across a wide range of plausible distances.

</details>


### [117] [FAST Pulsar Database IV. Spike subpulses and quasi-periodic subpulses of 25 pulsars observed by FAST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23970)
*Tao Wang,N. N. Cai,J. L. Han,W. C. Jing,Z. L. Yang,Y. Yan,D. J. Zhou,P. F. Wang,C. Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用FAST望远镜的高时间分辨率（49微秒）观测，在21颗脉冲星中检测到大量尖峰子脉冲（spike subpulses），在13颗脉冲星中检测到准周期子脉冲（quasi-periodic subpulses）。尖峰子脉冲通常具有强线极化特性，可能是子脉冲的基本发射单元；准周期子脉冲的特征周期约为零点几毫秒，并探究了其与脉冲星自转周期的可能相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高时间分辨率和高灵敏度观测可以揭示脉冲星单个脉冲的精细结构，但目前对此类结构的检测和特征分析尚不充分。

Method: 使用FAST望远镜以49微秒的时间分辨率观测脉冲星数据，检测尖峰子脉冲和准周期子脉冲，并分析其极化特性和周期特征。

Result: 从21颗脉冲星中检测到大量尖峰子脉冲（FAST时间分辨率下难以或刚好分辨），从13颗脉冲星中检测到准周期子脉冲，其特征周期约为零点几毫秒。

Conclusion: 尖峰子脉冲普遍具有强线极化，可能是子脉冲的基本发射单元；准周期子脉冲的特征周期可能与脉冲星自转周期存在相关性。

Abstract: Fine structures of individual pulses can be detected when observations are conducted with a high time resolution and a great sensitivity. We examined pulsar data observed by the Five-hundred-metre Aperture Spherical radio Telescope (FAST) with a time resolution of 49~{\textmu}s, and detected a large number of spike subpulses of 21 pulsars and quasi-periodic subpulses from 13 pulsars. These spike subpulses cannot be or are marginally resolved by the FAST observation time resolution, and are generally strongly linearly polarized, which may be primary emission elements of subpulses. For the quasi-periodic subpulses from 13 pulsars, we measured their characteristic periods, generally a few tenths of a millisecond, and examined their possible correlation with pulsar rotation period.

</details>


### [118] [Radio eclipse of the slowest spinning Galactic-field spider pulsar PSR J1932+2121 and its X-ray emission prospect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24069)
*Ze-Xin Du,Yun-Wei Yu,Zong-Lin Yang,Aming Chen,Peng-Fei Wang,Jin-Lin Han,De-Jiang Zhou,Xiao-Peng You*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过FAST发现的蜘蛛脉冲星PSR J1932+2121，利用轨道相位依赖的色散测量变化和流量轮廓建模，约束了系统倾角（约88.55°）和低质量主序伴星的弱恒星风特性。研究发现同步辐射吸收机制可解释食变现象，并预测其X射线通量在伴星下合附近可被XMM/EPIC等探测，且呈现多普勒致亮的双峰轨道调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜘蛛脉冲星PSR J1932+2121具有明显的射电食现象，是研究食机制和激波高能辐射的理想实验室，但目前对其系统几何和辐射机制尚不明确。

Method: 建立轨道相位依赖的色散测量变化和流量轮廓模型，结合风相互作用和激波几何，约束系统倾角和伴星风参数；通过同步辐射吸收机制拟合食变观测数据；预测激波同步辐射的X射线流量和轨道调制特征。

Result: 系统倾角约88.55°（近边缘视），伴星为低质量主序星且具有弱恒星风；同步辐射吸收可再现观测流量变化；预测X射线在伴星下合附近可探测，具双峰轨道调制。

Conclusion: 建立了该脉冲星食机制和激波辐射的理论框架，为未来多波段观测提供了指导。

Abstract: PSR J1932$+$2121 is a newly discovered spider pulsar with a pronounced radio eclipse identified by the Five-hundred-meter Aperture Spherical radio Telescope (FAST); it provides an ideal laboratory for studying the eclipse mechanism and high-energy emission from its intrabinary shock (IBS). By modeling the orbital-phase-dependent dispersion measure variations and flux profiles during the eclipse region with the wind interaction and IBS geometry, we constrain the system to a nearly edge-on inclination ($i_{\mathrm{o}} \simeq 88.55^{\circ}$) and a weak stellar wind from a low-mass main-sequence companion. Our analysis of the eclipse mechanism suggests that synchrotron absorption by nonthermal electrons can reproduce the observed flux variations with reasonable parameters for the eclipsing medium. We further predict the synchrotron emission from the IBS in PSR J1932$+$2121, showing that its X-ray flux, particularly near the inferior conjunction of the companion star, could be detectable with XMM/EPIC, EP/FXT, or eXTP/SFA and should exhibit double-peaked orbital modulation by Doppler boosting. These results provide a theoretical framework for understanding this system and for guiding future multiwavelength probes of spider pulsars.

</details>


### [119] [EP251023a: A fast X-ray transient featuring a magnetar-powered optical internal plateau followed by a steep decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24329)
*Shuai-Qing Jiang,Dong Xu,Wei-Hua Lei,Jie An,Yuan-Chuan Zou,Zi-Pei Zhu,Ryan Chornock,Dmitry Svinkin,Wen-Xiong Li,E. Fernández-García,Yue Wu,Wen-Da Zhang,Shao-Yu Fu,Xing Liu,Lin-Bo He,Moira Andrews,A. J. Castro-Tirado,Joseph R. Farah,Dmitry Frederiks,M. Gritsevich,D. Andrew Howell,Ding-Fang Hu,Alexandra L. Lysenko,A. Maury,Curtis McCully,S. B. Pandey,I. Pérez-García,Anna Ridnaia,Anastasia Tsvetkova,Mikhail Ulanov,S. -Y. Wu,Kathryn Wynn,D. -R. Xiong,Hao-Nan Yang,B. -B. Zhang,Tong Zhao*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: EP251023a是一个仅由EP探测到的河外快速X射线暂现源（eFXT），无伽马射线对应体。其主发射持续约292秒，后续长时尾发射持续至1571秒。红移z=2.232，来自Keck光谱的强吸收特征。晚期光变曲线呈现消色差平台随后极陡衰减（斜率3.99），与快速旋转毫秒磁星引擎模型一致。各向同性风和喷流准直场景下分别给出了磁星初始周期和磁场强度的约束。该源与GRB 070707可能代表一类罕见的光学波段磁星驱动内部平台事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 报道和表征一个特殊的无伽马射线对应体的河外快速X射线暂现源EP251023a，利用多波段观测数据（EP、Konus-Wind上限、Keck光谱、SED）限制其物理起源，并探讨其作为光学波段磁星驱动内部平台事件新样本的意义。

Method: 利用EP卫星探测eFXT事件并获取X射线光变曲线；结合Konus-Wind上限约束伽马射线各向同性能量；通过Keck光谱识别吸收特征并确定红移；基于宽带SED分析晚期光变曲线的平台与陡降特征；采用磁星引擎模型拟合数据，分别在各向同性风和各向异性喷流（张角0.1 rad）场景下约束初始周期P₀和磁场强度Bₚ。

Result: 主发射T₉₀=292±19 s，尾发射持续至1571 s；伽马射线各向同性能量上限E_{γ,iso}≤5.7×10⁵² erg；红移z=2.232±0.001；晚期光变曲线在约49 ks处出现断点，陡降斜率3.99；各向同性风场景下P₀<2.27 ms、Bₚ<8.33×10¹⁴ G；喷流场景下P₀<32.15 ms、Bₚ<1.18×10¹⁶ G。

Conclusion: EP251023a的观测特征与快速旋转毫秒磁星引擎模型一致，其光学波段的内部平台与GRB 070707类似，可能代表一类罕见的光学磁星驱动内部平台事件，与以往主要在X射线中探测到的此类事件不同。未来发现更多类似事件有助于厘清光学波段与X射线波段中磁星驱动内部发射之间的关系。

Abstract: EP251023a is an extragalactic fast X-ray transient (eFXT) detected solely by EP without a gamma-ray counterpart. The prompt emission consists of a main emission with a duration $T_{90}=292\pm19$ s, followed by a long-lasting tail emission that persists until the observation ends at $T_0+1571$ s. With the upper limit of Konus--Wind, we derived a conservative upper limit on the isotropic gamma-ray energy $E_{γ,\rm{iso}}$ of $5.7 \times 10^{52}$ erg for the main emission phase. A redshift of $z = 2.232\pm0.001$ is identified from strong absorption features in the Keck spectrum, which also indicate a relatively low host-galaxy HI column density. Based on the broadband spectral energy distribution, the late-time light curves show an achromatic plateau, followed by an extremely steep decay with a slope of 3.99 after a break at about 49 ks, which is consistent with a rapidly spinning millisecond magnetar engine. Under the isotropic wind scenario, we obtain the initial period $P_0<2.27$~ms and the magnetic field strength $B_p<8.33\times10^{14}$~G for the magnetar; whereas considering a jet collimation with a typical opening angle of 0.1 rad relaxes these constraints to $P_0<32.15$~ms and $B_p<1.18\times10^{16}$~G. Together with GRB\,070707, EP251023a may represent a rare class of optical magnetar-powered internal plateaus with little external-shock contamination, unlike previous examples detected primarily in X-rays. Future discoveries of similar events will help clarify the relationship between magnetar-powered internal emission observed in the optical band and that detected only in X-rays.

</details>


### [120] [Asteroseismology of neutron stars with both hyperons and $Δ$ resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24343)
*Hao Sun,Jia-Xing Niu,Yong Gao,Hong-Bo Li,Cheng-Jun Xia*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文研究了超子和Δ共振态对中子星非径向振荡频率的影响，发现Δ共振态显著增强g-mode频率，对探测中子星内部组成有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究超子和Δ共振态对中子星非径向振荡频率的影响，以理解中子星内部组成并解释最新观测数据。

Method: 采用多种相对论性能量密度泛函描述核子-核子相互作用，对Δ-介子耦合采用普适耦合方案，在Cowling近似和完全广义相对论框架下计算非径向振荡频率。

Result: Δ共振态对中子星容纳最新质量和半径观测（PSR J0030+0451等）至关重要；Δ共振区的出现使g-mode振荡能量集中在该区域，g-mode频率显著增大；f-mode和p-mode受Δ共振影响较小；Cowling近似对f-mode误差约20%，g-mode误差在10%以内。

Conclusion: Δ共振态对中子星g-mode频率具有显著影响，这为探测中子星内部组成提供了重要探针。

Abstract: Employing various relativistic energy density functionals for nucleon-nucleon interactions, we investigate the impact of hyperons and $Δ$ resonances on the frequencies of non-radial oscillations in neutron stars, where the universal coupling scheme is adopted for the $Δ$-meson couplings. It is found that the inclusion of $Δ$ resonances is essential for neutron stars to accommodate the recent mass and radius measurements of PSR J0030+0451, PSR J0740+6620, PSR J0437-4715, PSR J0614-3329, and HESS J1731-347. As $Δ$ resonances start to emerge in neutron stars' inner core, the $g$-mode oscillation energy becomes concentrated within the $Δ$-admixed region, leading to a sharp increase in the $g$-mode frequency. We also examine the $f$-mode and $p$-mode oscillations and find that the impact of $Δ$ resonances on these modes is less pronounced than the $g$-modes. The oscillation frequencies calculated in the Cowling approximation are then compared with those from full general relativity, confirming that the Cowling approximation introduces an error of approximately 20\% for the $f$-modes and within 10\% for the $g$-modes. The notable effect of $Δ$ resonances on neutron stars' $g$-mode frequencies holds important implications for probing the internal composition of neutron stars.

</details>


### [121] [Magnetized Coulomb crystals in neutron star crusts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24355)
*Cheng-Jun Xia,Toshiki Maruyama,Nobutoshi Yasutake,Toshitaka Tatsumi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用三维周期边界条件，结合Thomas-Fermi近似和等效磁荷方法，研究了中子星壳层中磁化Coulomb晶体的性质。结果表明：随离子振动宽度b增大晶体软化；马德隆常数KM在低磁场下波动，高磁场下先增后减；BCC晶格在Bz0<3×10^15 G时略稳定，FCC在更高磁场下可能更稳定；弹性常数在中等磁场增长，极高磁场下归零，超过10^16 G时难以识别稳定晶格结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强磁场对中子星壳层中Coulomb晶体性质的影响，特别是磁场如何改变晶格的稳定性、马德隆常数和弹性常数，以揭示强磁场在致密星物质结构塑造中的作用。

Method: 采用全三维周期性边界条件，通过Thomas-Fermi近似固定电子密度分布，使用等效磁荷方法处理非均匀磁场。核子用高斯波函数描述，宽度b包含零点振动和有限温度效应。研究对象为12^C组成的Coulomb晶体，离子数密度nd=10^{-9} fm^{-3}，置于不同外磁场下。

Result: (1) 晶体随b增大而软化；(2) 马德隆常数KM在Bz0≤3×10^14 G时波动，随后增加至约3×10^15 G后下降；(3) BCC晶格在Bz0<3×10^15 G时略稳定于FCC，更高磁场时FCC可能更稳定；(4) 弹性常数c11-c12和c44在3×10^14~2×10^15 G范围内增长，随后下降趋零；(5) Bz0≳10^16 G时难以识别稳定晶格结构。

Conclusion: 强磁场显著影响中子星壳层中Coulomb晶体的稳定性与弹性性质：中等强度磁场可增强晶格稳定性，而极高磁场（≥10^16 G）会导致晶格结构难以维持。这些结果为理解强磁化致密星（如磁星）的内部结构提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We investigate the properties of magnetized Coulomb crystals in neutron star crusts using a fully three-dimensional geometry with periodic boundary conditions. The electron density profiles are fixed via the Thomas-Fermi approximation, and the nonuniform magnetic fields are treated with the equivalent magnetic charge method. The study focuses on Coulomb crystals composed of $^{12}$C at an ion number density $n_d = 10^{-9}\ \text{fm}^{-3}$, subjected to various external magnetic fields. Nuclei are described by a Gaussian wave function, where the width $b$ encapsulates the effects of zero-point ion vibrations and finite temperature. Our findings show that the crystal softens as $b$ increases. The Madelung constant $K_M$ fluctuates with the external magnetic field $B_{z0}$ at $B_{z0}\leq 3\times 10^{14}$ G. At higher field strengths, $K_M$ increases until $B_{z0} \approx 3\times 10^{15}$ G and then decreases. The body-centered cubic (BCC) lattice is slightly more stable than the face-centered cubic (FCC) lattice when $B_{z0} < 3\times 10^{15}$ G, whereas the FCC lattice may become more stable at larger $B_{z0}$. The elastic constants $c_{11}-c_{12}$ and $c_{44}$ are computed and tabulated, which grow with $B_{z0}$ for $3\times 10^{14}\ \mathrm{G}\lesssim B_{z0}\lesssim 2\times 10^{15}$ G and then decline toward zero as the field strength increases further. For $B_{z0}\gtrsim 10^{16}$ G, it becomes difficult to identify a stable lattice structure. These results provide valuable insights into the role of strong magnetic fields in shaping the properties of Coulomb crystals in compact stars.

</details>


### [122] [Probing Merger Shocks in Galaxy Clusters in the SKA Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24738)
*Arpan Pal,Ruta Kale,Gabriella Di Gennaro,Francesco de Gasperin,Swarna Chatterjee,Majidul Rahaman,Ramananda Santra,Mamta Pandey-Pommier,Abhirup Datta,Kenda Knowles,Nasmi S. Anand*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了星系团并合激波及其射电遗迹的观测与理论进展，重点介绍了SKA先导望远镜的成果，并展望了SKA低/中频阵列结合X射线观测在未来揭示激波微观物理和宇宙演化方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系团并合释放巨大的引力势能，驱动激波和湍流，产生Mpc尺度的射电遗迹，但其背后的物理机制和时间演化等基本问题尚未解决。

Method: 综述SKA先导和前期望远镜的观测证据，将射电遗迹与激波联系起来，讨论相关现象（射电凤凰、温和再加速尾），并规划SKA-Low/Mid结合X射线观测的研究方向。

Result: SKA从AA0.5到AA4的分阶段部署能力已明确，并识别了可用于揭示并合激波物理的科学验证目标。

Conclusion: 通过单个目标的连续谱和偏振精细研究以及宽场巡天的遗迹群体统计，SKA时代将对并合激波的微观物理和宇宙演化提供重要洞察。

Abstract: Galaxy cluster mergers represent the most energetic phenomena in the Universe since the Big Bang releasing gravitational potential energy of $\sim 10^{63-64}$ erg, injecting turbulence and driving shocks through the intracluster medium (ICM). These merger shocks can accelerate cosmic ray electrons and compress magnetic fields, sometimes producing Mpc-scale synchrotron radio structures known as radio relics. Radio relics are powerful tracers of merger dynamics, particle acceleration, and magnetic field evolution, yet fundamental questions about the underlying physics and their time evolution remain unresolved. In this chapter, we review the current understanding of cluster merger shocks and their radio signatures, presenting the observational evidence from the SKA pathfinders and precursors, linking radio relics to shocks alongside outstanding theoretical challenges. We also consider related shock-influenced phenomena: radio phoenices from revived fossil AGN plasma and Gently Re-Energised Tails. Further, we outline the directions of investigation using the sensitivities of the SKA-Low and Mid complemented with X-ray observations that will allow us to make significant progress in understanding the cluster merger shocks. Detailed studies of individual targets in continuum and polarization and studies of populations of relics using wide surveys will both provide insights to the micro-physics and cosmic evolution of merger shocks. We present SKAO capabilities across staged deployments starting from AA0.5 to AA4 and identify science verification targets that will illuminate the physics of cluster merger shocks in the SKA era

</details>
